> सखे॑व॒ सख्ये॑ गातु॒वित्त॑मो भव ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । सः । मदानां । पते। इंदो इति । देवप्पराः । असि ।  
> सखाइव । सख्ये । गातुवित् 5 तमः । भव ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 38

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नोऽस्मदादीनां मदानां मदकानां पते स्वामिन् हे इंदो सोम स
> त्वं देवप्परा असि । प्सर इति रूपनाम । दीप्तरूपो भवसि । स त्वं सखेव यथा
> सखा सुहृत् सख्ये मित्राय सत्यं मार्गं ज्ञापयति तद्वदस्माकमपि गातुवित्तमोऽत्यंतं मार्गस्य लंभको भव । ज्ञापको भव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O lord of our intoxicating drinks, O Indu, Soma, you are देवप्परा (*devapparā*); प्सर (*psara*) is a word for form, so you are of shining form. As a friend shows the true path to a friend, so be for us too the best finder of the path, the one who makes it known.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- मदानां (*madānāṃ*)—for joys
- पते (*pate*)—lord
- इंदो (*iṃdo*)—O Soma
- सः (*saḥ*)—you, the famous one
- देवप्परा (*devapparā*)—having a shining form
- असि (*asi*)—you are
- सख्ये (*sakhye*)—to a friend
- सखेव (*sakheva*)—as a friend is a guide
- गातुवित्तमः (*gātuvittamaḥ*)—becoming the best guide for us
- भव (*bhava*)—be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, lord of our joys, you, the famous one, are of shining form. As a friend is a guide to a friend, be for us an excellent guide.

##### English translation
Lord of our exhilaration, Indu, you are of the brilliant form ; be our true gnide as a friend is to a friend.
#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सने॑मि कृ॒ध्य१॒॑गस्मदा र॒क्षसं॒ कं चि॑द॒त्रिणं॑ ।  
> अपादे॑वं द्व॒युमंहो॑ युयोधि नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सनेमि ! कृधि । अस्मत् । आ । रक्षसं । कं । चित् । अत्रिणं ।  
> आप । अदेवं । द्वयुं । अंहः । युयोधि । नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम अस्मदस्मासु सनेमि । पुराणनामैतत् । पुराणं सख्यं कृधि । कुरु।
> आ अपि चात्रिणमदनशीलं कं चिद्रक्षसमपयुध्यस्व । कीदृशं । आदेवमदेवनशीलं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, सनेमि (*sanemi*) is a word meaning "old": make (कृधि (*kṛdhi*), do) the old friendship with us. And fight off some devouring Rakṣas, one who is godless, द्वयुं (*dvayuṃ*), that is possessed of a twofold nature, joined with truth and untruth, or possessed of outer and inner illusion.
### Printed p. 39

> द्वयुं द्वयवंतं सत्यानृतयुक्तं बाह्याभ्यंतरमायायुक्तं वा । किं च नोऽस्माकमंहः पापं चाप युयोधि । संप्रहर ॥ युध्यतेर्बहुलं छंदसीति शपः
> श्नुः । वा छंदसीति हेर्णत्वाभावाद्गुणः । धलोपश्रांदसः । यौतीर्वा श्ला
> पूर्णवद्रूपं :

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Further, drive away also our sin (अंहः (*aṃhaḥ*)). The remainder of this block is grammatical (the form युयोधि (*yuyodhi*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—in us
- सनेमि (*sanemi*)—the ancient friendship
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—continue
- आ (*ā*)—and
- अत्रिणं (*atriṇaṃ*)—all-devouring
- कं चित् रक्षसं (*kaṃ cit rakṣasaṃ*)—whatever Rākṣasa there may be
- अदेनं (*adenaṃ*)—one who is not devoted to the gods
- द्वयुं (*dvayuṃ*)—and a "fivefold" one **NOTE**
- अप युयोधि (*apa yuyodhi*)—destroy
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- *aṃhar*—sins
- अप (युयोधि) (*apa (yuyodhi)*)—destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, continue the ancient friendship with us. Destroy whatever all-devouring Rākṣasa there may be, the one without devotion to the gods, the "fivefold" one. Destroy our sins.

##### English translation
Show us (your) ancient (friendship); (drive off) the voracious Rakshasa, the impious, the double dealer—drive away our sin.
## Sūkta 105 (Maṇḍala 9)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तं व इति षडृचं द्वितीयं सूक्तं । पर्वतनारदयोरार्षं । अन्यत्पूर्ववत् ।
> तं व इत्यनुक्रांतं ॥ गतः सूक्तविनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

तं व (*taṃ va*) is a hymn of six Ṛks, the second hymn. Its seer is Parvata and Nārada; the rest is as before. It is listed in the Anukramaṇī as तं व (*taṃ va*). The use of the hymn has been stated.

##### Anuvāda

This hymn beginning तं वः (*taṃ vaḥ*) is the second hymn in the seventh anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. As for the preceding hymn, the two seers are Parvata and Nārada. The seer, deity and metre are as in the previous hymn. In the Anukramaṇī it is given as तं वः (*taṃ vaḥ*). The viniyoga was stated earlier.

Hymn 105. Maṇḍala 9; Anuvāka 7; Sūkta 105. Aṣṭaka 7; Adhyāya 5; Varga 8. Number of Ṛks in the hymn: 6. Seers: Parvata and Nārada. Deity: Pavamāna Soma. Metre: Uṣṇih.
### Printed p. 40
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं वः॑ सखायो॒ मदा॑य पुना॒नम॒भि गा॑यत ।  
> शिशुं॒ न य॒ज्ञैः स्व॑दयंत गू॒र्तिभिः॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं ! वः । सखायः । मदाय । पुनानं । अभि । गायत ।  
> शिशुं । न : यज्ञैः । स्वदयंत । गूर्तिबिः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सखाय ऋत्विजः वो यूयं मदाय देवानां मदार्थं पुनानं
> पूयमानं तं सोममभि गायत । अभिष्टुत । तमिमं सोमं शिशुं न शिशुमिवालंकारैः क्षीरादिभिश्च यथा स्वादूकुर्वंति शद्वद्यज्ञैर्हविर्भिर्मिश्रणैर्गूर्तिभिः
> स्तुतिभिश्च स्वदयंत । स्वादूकुर्वंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O friends, priests: you, for the exhilaration of the gods, sing to that Soma who is being purified. Just as people sweeten a child with ornaments and with milk and the like, so sweeten him (स्वदयंत (*svadayaṃta*), make him sweet) with sacrifices, that is with oblations and mixings, and with praises (गूर्तिभिः (*gūrtibhiḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*)—O priests who are friends
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for the joy of the gods
- पुनानं (*punānaṃ*)—who has been purified
- तं (*taṃ*)—Soma
- अभिगायत (*abhigāyata*)—praise in song
- शिशुं न (*śiśuṃ na*)—as mothers adorn their children
- यज्ञैः (*yajñaiḥ*)—with oblations and the like, the means of sacrifice
- गूर्तिभिः (*gūrtibhiḥ*)—with praises
- स्वदयंत (*svadayaṃta*)—adorn

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, friends, sing in praise of the purified Soma for the joy of the gods. As mothers adorn their children, adorn him with oblations and the like, the means of sacrifice, and with praises.

##### English translation
Hymn, friends, the Soma purified for the exhilaration (of the gods) gratify him with oblations and praises as (men gratify) a child (with ornaments).
### Printed p. 41
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ५० वत्स इव मातृभिःमूर्तिन्वानो अज्यते ।  
> दे॒वा॒वीर्मदो॑ म॒तिभिः॒ परि॑ष्कृतः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सं, वत्स्य इव मातृभिः इंदु, हिन्वानः । अज्यते  
> देव्य अवीः मदः मतिभिः : परिकृतः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रवर्गेऽभिष्टवे सं वत्स इव मातृभिरित्येका । सूत्रं च समि वत्समित्यनया
> सहोक्तं ॥
> हिन्वानः प्रेर्यमाणः इंदुः सोमो वसतीवरीभिः समज्यते । सम्यक्
> सिक्तो भवति । तत्र दृष्टांतः । वत्स इव वत्सो यथा मातृभिगर्गोभिः समक्तो
> भवति तद्वत् । कीदृशः । देवानीर्देवानां रक्षको मदो मदकरो मतिभिः
> स्तुतिभिः परिष्कृतोऽलंकृतः ॥ भूषणार्थे संपर्युपेभ्य इति सुडागमः । परिनिविभ्यः ।
> पा. ८-३-७० । इति सुटः षत्वं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the Pravargya, in the Abhiṣṭava, one Ṛk is सं वत्स इव मातृभिः (*saṃ vatsa iva mātṛbhiḥ*). The Sūtra gives it together with the Ṛk समि वत्सं (*sami vatsaṃ*). Indu, Soma, being impelled, is anointed together with the waters (वसतीवरीभिः (*vasatīvarībhiḥ*)), that is, is well sprinkled. The illustration: as a calf is anointed by its mothers, the cows. Of what kind is he? Protector of the gods, intoxicating, adorned (परिष्कृतः (*pariṣkṛtaḥ*)) by praises. The rest of the block is grammatical (the augment suṭ).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मातृभिः (*mātṛbhiḥ*)—by the cows, the mothers
- वत्स इव (*vatsa iva*)—as calves are cherished and nourished
- हिन्वानः (*hinvānaḥ*)—the pressed one
- देवावीः (*devāvīḥ*)—protector of the gods
- मदः (*madaḥ*)—the joy-giver
- मतिभिः (*matibhiḥ*)—by praises
- परिष्कृतः (*pariṣkṛtaḥ*)—adorned
- इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*)—Soma
- समज्यते (*samajyate*)—is made well refined

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As calves are cherished and licked by their mothers the cows, so Soma, the pressed one, protector of the gods, the one who causes pride **NOTE**, adorned by praises, is made well refined.

##### English translation
Indu being sent forth is sprinkled (with waters) as a calf (is nourished) by its mother—the protector of the deities, the exhilarator, graced by praises.
### Printed p. 42

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Abhiṣṭava of the sacrifice called Pravargya this Ṛk, सं वत्स इव मातृभिः (*saṃ vatsa iva mātṛbhiḥ*), has its viniyoga; this was stated where the viniyoga of the Ṛk समि वत्सं (*sami vatsaṃ*) was given (9-104-2).
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं दक्षा॑य॒ साध॑नो॒ऽयं शर्धा॑य वी॒तये॑ ।  
> अ॒यं दे॒वेभ्यो॒ मधु॑मत्तमः सु॒तः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं दक्षाय साधनः । अयं । शर्धाय वीतये ।  
> अयं देवेभ्यः । मधुमत् 5 तमः सुतः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अयं सोमो दक्षाय बलाय वर्धनाय ना साधनः साधयिता भवति ।
> तथायं सोमः शर्धाय वेगार्थं वीतये देवानां भक्षणार्थं च भवति ।
> सुतोऽभिषुतोऽयं सोमो देवेभ्य इंद्रादिभ्यो मधुमत्तमोऽतिशयेन
> माधुर्ययुक्तो भवति । अत्यंतं मदकरो भवतीति वा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Soma becomes the accomplisher for दक्षाय (*dakṣāya*), strength, that is for increase. Likewise he is for शर्धाय (*śardhāya*), speed, and वीतये (*vītaye*), for the gods' eating. Pressed, this Soma is for the gods, Indra and the others, मधुमत्तमः (*madhumattamaḥ*), exceedingly endowed with sweetness; or else, he is exceedingly intoxicating.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—that Soma
- दक्षाय (*dakṣāya*)—for strength
- साधनः (*sādhanaḥ*)—becomes the accomplisher
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Soma
- शर्धाय (*śardhāya*)—for speed
- वीतये (*vītaye*)—is fit for the drinking of the gods
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—pressed
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Soma
- देवेभ्यः (*devebhyaḥ*)—to the gods, Indra and others
- मधुमत्तमः (*madhumattamaḥ*)—is the greatest joy-giver

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Soma is an accomplisher of strength. This Soma is fit for exceeding speed and for the drinking of the gods. This pressed Soma is the greatest joy-giver to the gods, Indra and others.

##### English translation
This (Soma) is an instrument for invigoration; he (is useful) for speed (and) for the banquet of your gods ; he is effused most sweet-flavoured for the gods-

### Printed p. 43
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> गोम॑न्न इंदो॒ अश्व॑वत्सु॒तः सु॑दक्ष धन्व ।  
> शुचिं॑ ते॒ वर्ण॒मधि॒ गोषु॑ दीधरं ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> गोऽमत् । नः । इंदो इति । अश्ववत् । सुतः । सुदक्ष । धन्व ।  
> शुचिं । ते । वर्णं । अधि । गोषु । दीधरु ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सुदक्ष सुबल हे इंदो सोम सुतोऽभिषुतस्त्वं नोऽस्माकं
> गोमद्यज्ञसाधनगोयुक्तमश्ववदश्वयुक्तं धनं धन्व । गमय ॥ धविर्गत्यर्थो
> भूवादिः॥ ततोऽहं शुचिं पूतं दीप्यमानं वा ते तव वर्णं रसं गोषु गव्येषु
> क्षीरादिष्वधि धीधरं । अधिधारयामि । मिश्रयामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Sudakṣa, mighty Indu (Soma), being pressed out, grant us wealth that has cattle (the cattle that serve as means of the sacrifice) and horses; धन्व (*dhanva*) means "cause to go", from the root धवि (*dhavi*) meaning motion, of the first (bhū) class. Then I mix your pure or shining colour (juice) with the cows' products, milk and the like (*adhi dhīdharaṃ*, "I hold over, I mix").

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुदक्ष (*sudakṣa*)—one of exceeding strength
- इंदो (*iṃdo*)—O Soma-deva
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—you who are pressed
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- गोमत् (*gomat*)—that which has cattle
- अश्ववत् (*aśvavat*)—that which has horses
- धन्व (*dhanva*)—provide
- शुचिं (*śuciṃ*)—shining
- ते (*te*)—your
- वर्णं (*varṇaṃ*)—juice
- गोषु अधि (*goṣu adhi*)—with milk and the like
- दीधरं (*dīdharaṃ*)—I mix

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, of exceeding strength, being pressed, provide us wealth consisting of cattle and horses. I myself mix your shining juice with milk and the like.

##### English translation
Powerful Indu who are effused, pour upon us ( wealth ) comprising cattle and horses ; I mix the pure juice with the curds and milk.

### Printed p. 44
#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स नो॑ हरीणां पत॒ इंदो॑ दे॒वप्स॑रस्तमः ।  
> सखे॑व॒ सख्ये॒ नर्यो॑ रु॒चे भ॑व ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । हरीणां । पते । इंदो इति । देवप्सरःतमः ।  
> सखाऽइव । सख्ये । नर्यः । रुचे । भव ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे हरीणां पते नोऽस्मदीयानां हरितवर्णानां पशूनां स्वामिन् हे
> इंदो सोम देवप्सरस्तमोऽतिशयेन दीप्तरूपोपेतः स त्वं नर्यः कर्मनेतृभ्य
> ऋत्विग्भो हितः स त्वं नोऽस्माकं रुचे भव । दीप्तिकरो भव । किमिव । सखेव
> यथा सखा सख्ये मित्राय दीप्तिकरो भवति तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O lord of our green-coloured cattle, O Indu Soma, of a form exceedingly bright, being beneficial (नर्यः (*naryaḥ*)) to the priests who lead the rites, be for our brightness; make us shine. How? As a friend becomes a bringer of brightness to a friend, so you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- हरीणां (*harīṇāṃ*)—of the green-coloured cattle
- पते (*pate*)—O lord
- इंदो (*iṃdo*)—O Soma-deva
- देवप्सरस्तमः (*devapsarastamaḥ*)—having a form endowed with exceeding brilliance
- सः (*saḥ*)—you
- नरः (*naraḥ*)—being beneficial to us
- सख्ये (*sakhye*)—to one who is a friend
- सखेव (*sakheva*)—as a friend becomes a helper
- (नः (*naḥ*)—our)
- रुचे (*ruce*)—as a helper for a bright life
- भव (*bhava*)—be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, lord of all our green-coloured cattle, you who have a form endowed with exceeding brilliance, being beneficial to us, be a helper to our bright life, as a friend is a helper to a friend.

##### English translation
Lord of our bay-coloured (cattle), Indu, who have a most brilliant form, you who are kind to the priests be for a light to us as a friend (gives light) to a friend.

### Printed p. 45
#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सने॑मि॒ त्वम॒स्मदा अदे॑वं॒ कं चि॑द॒त्रिणं॑ ।  
> सा॒ह्वा इं॑दो॒ परि॒ बाधो॒ अप॑ द्व॒युं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सनेमि ' त्वं ' अस्मत् । आ । अदेवं । कं । चित् । अत्रिणं ।  
> सहवान् । इंदो इति । परि । बाधः । अप । द्वयुं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम त्वं सनेमि पुराणं सख्यमस्मदस्मासु कुरु । आ अपि चादेवमदेवनशीलं कं चिदपृत्रिणमदनशीलं राक्षसमस्मदस्मत्तोऽपगमय। किं च हे इंदो
> साह्वाङ्छत्रूनभिभवन् बाधो बाधमानाप्परि जहि । तथा द्वयुं द्वयवंतं सत्यानृतयुक्तं बाह्याभ्यंतरमायाद्वयोपेतं वा राक्षसमस्मत्तोऽपगमय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, establish your ancient friendship (सनेमि (*sanemi*)) in us. Also drive away from us any Rākṣasa who is godless, not devoted to the gods, and devouring. Further, O Indu, overcoming enemies, strike down those who oppress us; and drive away from us the "double" (द्वयुं (*dvayuṃ*)) Rākṣasa, either one who is possessed of both truth and falsehood, or one possessed of two kinds of deceit, outer and inner.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Soma-deva) त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- सनेमि (*sanemi*)—the ancient friendship
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—in us, foster
- आ (*ā*)—and
- अदेवं (*adevaṃ*)—not a worshipper of the gods
- कं चित् (*kaṃ cit*)—whoever
- अत्रिणं (*atriṇaṃ*)—even if there be an Asura devouring all
- अप (*apa*)—destroy him
- इंदो (*iṃdo*)—O Soma-deva
- साह्वान् (*sāhvān*)—conquering enemies
- बाधः (*bādhaḥ*)—the oppressors
- द्वयुं (*dvayuṃ*)—the double-dealers
- परि (*pari*) (जहि (*jahi*))—defeat and destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, foster with us your ancient friendship. And whatever Asura there be who is not a worshipper of the gods and who devours all, destroy him. O Soma, being possessed of power to conquer our enemies, defeat and destroy our oppressors and double-dealers.

##### English translation
Do you (show) us your ancient (fiendship), drive away the impious voracious (Rakshasa); Indu who are victorious overcome those who oppress us, (drive) off the double-dealing (Rakshasa).

### Printed p. 46
## Sūkta 106 (Maṇḍala 9)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इंद्रमच्छेति चतुर्दशर्चं तृतीयं सूक्तमौष्ठिहं पवमादसोमदेवताकं । प्रथमस्य तृचस्य चक्षुराख्यपुत्रोऽग्निर्ऋषिः । द्वितीयस्य मनुपुत्रश्चक्षुर्नामा । अप्पुनाम्नः पुत्रो मनुस्तृतीयस्य । एवं नवर्चो गताः । शिष्टानामपि पंचानां चाक्षुषोऽग्निः । तथा चानुक्रम्यते । इंद्रमच्छ षळूनाग्निश्चाक्सुर्मानवो मनुराप्सव इति तृचाः पंचाग्निरिति ॥ गतो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The hymn इंद्रमच्छ (*iṃdramaccha*) has fourteen Ṛks and is the third hymn, addressed to Pavamāna Soma; the metre is Uṣṇih. For the first triplet the seer is Agni, son of Cakṣus; for the second, Cakṣus, son of Manu; for the third, Manu, son of Apsu. Thus nine verses are gone; for the remaining five the seer is Agni Cākṣuṣa. The Anukramaṇī is quoted accordingly: इंद्रमच्छ षळूनाग्निश्चाक्सुर्मानवो मनुराप्सव इति तृचाः पंचाग्निरिति (*iṃdramaccha ṣaḷūnāgniścāksurmānavo manurāpsava iti tṛcāḥ paṃcāgniriti*). The viniyoga has already been given.

##### Anuvāda

This hymn "Indramaccha" is the third hymn in the seventh anuvāka. It has fourteen Ṛks. The deity is Pavamāna Soma; the metre is Uṣṇik. For the first three Ṛks (1–3) the seer is Agni, son of the seer named Cakṣus. For Ṛks 4–6 the seer is Cakṣus, son of Manu; for Ṛks 7–9 the seer is Manu, son of Apsu. After these nine Ṛks, for the remaining five the seer is Agni, son of the seer Cakṣus. In the Anukramaṇī it is said: इंद्रमच्च षळूनाग्निश्चाक्षुपश्चक्षुर्मानवो मनुराप्सव इति तृचाः पंचाग्निः (*iṃdramacca ṣaḷūnāgniścākṣupaścakṣurmānavo manurāpsava iti tṛcāḥ paṃcāgniḥ*). The viniyoga has been stated earlier.

Sūkta 106. Maṇḍala 9; Anuvāka 7; Sūkta 106; Aṣṭaka 7; Adhyāya 5; Vargas 9, 10, 11. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 14.
Seer: 1–3 Agni Cākṣuṣa; 4–6 Cakṣur Mānava; 7–9 Manu Āpsava; 10–14 Agni.
Deity: Pavamāna Soma.
Metre: Uṣṇik.
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॒मच्छ॑ सु॒ता इ॒मे वृष॑णं यंतु॒ हर॑यः ।  
> श्रु॒ष्टी जा॒तास॒ इंद॑वः स्व॒र्विदः॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्रं ' अच्च । सुता । इमे । वृषणं । यंतु । हरयः !  
> श्रुष्टी, जातासः । इंदवः । स्वःविदः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 47

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> श्रुष्टी । श्रुष्टीति क्षिप्रनाम । क्षिप्रं जातासो जाता इंदवः पात्रेषु
> क्षरंतः स्वर्विदः सर्वज्ञा हरयो हरितवर्णाः सुता अभिषुता इमे सोमा वृषणं
> कामानां सेक्तारमिंद्रमच्छ यंतु । अभिगच्छंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

श्रुष्टी (*śruṣṭī*) is a name for "quickly". These Soma juices (इंदवः (*iṃdavaḥ*)), quickly born, flowing in the vessels, all-knowing, green-coloured, pressed out, should go towards Indra, the showerer, the sprinkler of desires.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- श्रुष्टी (*śruṣṭī*)—quickly
- जातासः (*jātāsaḥ*)—that are produced
- इंदवः (*iṃdavaḥ*)—that flow in the vessels
- स्वर्विदः (*svarvidaḥ*)—that possess all knowledge
- हरयः (*harayaḥ*)—that are green-coloured
- सुताः (*sutāḥ*)—that are pressed
- इमे (*ime*)—these Soma juices
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—the granter of wishes
- इंद्रं अच्छ (*iṃdraṃ accha*)—towards Indra
- यंतु (*yaṃtu*)—let them flow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May these Soma juices, quickly produced, flowing in the vessels, possessing all knowledge, green-coloured and pressed out, flow towards Indra, the granter of wishes.

##### English translation
May these effused green-tinted Soma juiees quickly generated, all-knowing, proceed to Indra the showerer (of benefits).
#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यं भरा॑य सान॒सिरिंद्रा॑य पवते सु॒तः ।  
> सोमो॒ जैत्र॑स्य चेतति॒ यथा॑ वि॒दे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं भराय । सानसिः इंद्राय पवते सुतः !  
> सोमः । जैत्रस्य चेतति यथा विदे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> भराय संग्रामाय तदर्थं सानसिर्भजनीयः सुतोऽभिसुतोऽयं सोम
> इंद्रायेंद्रार्थं पवते । ग्रहादिषु क्षरति । ततः सोमो जैत्रस्य ॥ क्रियाग्रहणं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

For the battle (भराय (*bharāya*)), for that purpose, this pressed Soma, सानसिः (*sānasiḥ*) ("to be served"), flows for Indra, into the cups and the like. Then Soma जैत्रस्य (*jaitrasya*): as the act of seizing is to be done, the object receives the designation sampradāna, and by "in the sense of the dative the genitive occurs variously" the sixth case is used.

### Printed p. 48

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> कर्तव्यमिति कर्मणः संप्रदानसंज्ञा चतुर्थ्यर्थे बहुलमिति षष्ठी ॥ जयशीलमिंद्रं
> चेतति । जानाति । यथेंद्रो विदे लोकैर्ज्ञायते तथा जानाति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

(Continuing) जैत्रस्य (*jaitrasya*) is the victorious Indra; चेतति (*cetati*) means "knows". As Indra is known by the people (विदे (*vide*)), so he knows.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- भराय (*bharāya*)—for the battle
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—pressed
- सानसिः (*sānasiḥ*)—venerable
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Soma-deva
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for Indra
- पवते (*pavate*)—flows in the vessels
- यथा (*yathā*)—in the way that
- विदे (*vide*)—Indra is known by the people of the world, so
- जैत्रस्य (*jaitrasya*)—the victorious Indra
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma-deva
- चेतति (*cetati*)—knows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma-deva, pressed for the battle and venerable, flows in the vessels for Indra. As Indra is known by all the people of the world, so Soma-deva too knows Indra's victoriousness.

##### English translation
This effused Soma adorable for battle, is filtered for Indra ; Soma thinks of the victorious (Indra) as he is known (in the world).
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्येदिंद्रो॒ मदे॒ष्वा ग्रा॒भं गृ॑भ्णीत सान॒सिं ।  
> वज्रं॑ च॒ वृष॑णं भर॒त्सम॑प्सु॒जित् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । इत् । इंद्रः । मदेसु आ । ग्राभं । गृभ्नीत । सानसिं ।  
> वज्रं । च : वृषणं । भरत् । सं । अप्पुऽजित् ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आ अनंतरमिंद्रोऽस्येदस्य सोमस्यैव मदेषु संजातेषु सानसिं सर्वैः
> संभजनीयं ग्राभं ग्राहं ग्रहीतव्यं धनुर्गृभ्नित । गृह्णाति ॥ हृग्रहोर्भ इति
> भत्वं ॥ किंचाप्सुजिदुदकार्थं वृत्रस्य जेता । यद्वा । आप इत्यंतरिक्षनाम । अंतरिक्षेऽहिनामकस्य जेता । इंद्रो वृषणं वर्षितारं वज्रं च सु भरत् । सुबिभर्तु ॥
> बिभर्तेर्लेट्यडागमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Thereafter Indra, in the exhilarations (मदेषु (*madeṣu*)) arisen from this very Soma, takes the bow (ग्राभं (*grābhaṃ*), that which is to be taken), to be shared in by all (सानसिं (*sānasiṃ*)). Moreover *apsujit* is the conqueror of Vṛtra for the sake of water. Or else: *āpaḥ* is a name of the atmosphere; he is the conqueror of the one named Ahi in the atmosphere. Indra bears the showerer, the rain-giver, and the thunderbolt.

### Printed p. 49

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- अस्येत् आ (*asyet ā*)—produced from the drinking of this Soma
- मदेषु (*madeṣu*)—at the time of exhilaration
- सानसिं (*sānasiṃ*)—worthy of worship by all
- ग्राभं (*grābhaṃ*)—the bow
- गृभ्नीत (*gṛbhnīta*)—takes hold of
- अप्सुजित् (*apsujit*)—Indra, who conquered Vṛtra for the sake of the waters
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—granting desired ends
- वज्रं च (*vajraṃ ca*)—and the thunderbolt weapon
- सं भरत् (*saṃ bharat*)—may he hold

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

At the time of exhilaration produced by drinking this Soma, Indra takes hold of the bow, which is worthy of worship by all. May Indra, who conquered Vṛtra for the sake of the waters, also hold the thunderbolt weapon that grants desired ends.

##### English translation
In the Soma's exhilaration may Indra Seize hold of wealth to be enjoyed (by all) and conquering Vritra in the waters may he wield the thunderbolt the showerer (of blessings).

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र ध॑न्वा सोम॒ जागृ॑वि॒रिंद्रा॑येंदो॒ परि॑ स्रव ।  
> द्यु॒मंतं॒ शुष्॒मा भ॑रा स्व॒र्विदं॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । धन्व । सोम । जागृविः . इंद्राय । इंदो इति । परि । स्रव ।  
> द्युमंतं . शुष्मं । आ : भर । स्वःविदं ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम जागृविर्जागरणशीलत्वं प्र धन्व । प्रक्षर । हे इंदो सोम
> इंद्राय परि स्रव । परितः पात्रेषु क्षर । किं च द्युमंतं दीप्तियुक्तं स्वर्विदं
> सर्वस्य लंभकं शुष्मं शत्रूणां शोषकं बलमा भर । आहर ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, you who are ever wakeful, flow forth. O Indu (Soma), stream around for Indra, flowing into the vessels on every side. Bring also the strength (शुष्मं (*śuṣmaṃ*)) that is radiant, that obtains everything (स्वर्विदं (*svarvidaṃ*)) and that dries up enemies.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O Soma-deva
- जागृविः (*jāgṛviḥ*)—you who are always wakeful
- प्र धन्व (*pra dhanva*)—flow forth
- इंदोः (*iṃdoḥ*)—O Soma
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for Indra's drinking
- परि स्रव (*pari srava*)—

### Printed p. 50
- flow
- द्युमंतं (*dyumaṃtaṃ*)—radiant
- स्वर्विदं (*svarvidaṃ*)—leading to heaven
- शुष्मं (*śuṣmaṃ*)—and enemy-drying, the strength
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—supply to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, you who are ever wakeful, flow forth. Stream for Indra's drinking. Supply us with strength that is radiant, leads to heaven and dries up enemies.

##### English translation
Flow Soma who are vigilant ; Indu, spread around for Indra ; bring brilliant overpowering all-obtaining (strength)-

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्रा॑य॒ वृष॑णं॒ मदं॒ पव॑स्व वि॒श्वद॑र्शतः ।  
> स॒हस्र॑यामा पथि॒कृद्वि॑चक्ष॒णः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्राय । वृषणं । मदं । पवस्व । विश्वदर्शतः ।  
> सहस्रयामा । पथिकृत् । विचक्षणः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम त्वं वृषणं वर्षितारं मदं मदहेतुं रसमिंद्रायेंद्रार्थं
> पवस्व । क्षर । कीदृशः । विश्वदर्शतः सर्वैर्दर्शनीयः सहस्रयामा बहुमार्गः
> पथिकृद्यजमानानां सन्मार्गकरणशीलो विचक्षणः सर्वस्य विद्रष्टा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, flow for Indra the showering (वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)) exhilarating juice, the cause of exhilaration. What kind are you? विश्वदर्शतः (*viśvadarśataḥ*): to be seen by all; सहस्रयामा (*sahasrayāmā*): having many paths; पथिकृत् (*pathikṛt*): by nature one who makes a good path for the sacrificers; विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*): the seer of everything.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Soma-deva) विश्वदर्शतः (*viśvadarśataḥ*)—visible to all
- सहस्रयामा (*sahasrayāmā*)—possessing many paths
- पथिकृत् (*pathikṛt*)—maker of a good path for the sacrificers
- विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*)—of subtle vision; you, such as you are
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—granting desired ends
- मदं (*madaṃ*)—producing joy; the juice
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for Indra's drinking
- पवस्व (*pavasva*)—make flow

### Printed p. 51

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, you who are visible to all, have many paths, make a good path for the sacrificers and have subtle vision: cause the juice that grants desired ends and produces joy to flow for Indra's drinking.

##### English translation
Pour forth for Indra the exhilarating (juice) showering (benefits), you who are very beautiful, who have many paths, the finder of the (right) path, the observer (of all).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्मभ्यं॑ गातु॒वित्त॑मो दे॒वेभ्यो॒ मधु॑मत्तमः ।  
> स॒हस्रं॑ याहि प॒थिभिः॒ कनि॑क्रदत् ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्मभ्यं । गातुवित् ऽतमः । देवेभ्यः । मधुमत् ऽतमः ।  
> सहस्रं । याहि । पथिभिः । कनिक्रदत् ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम अस्मभ्यं गातुवित्तमोऽत्यंतं मार्गस्य लंभकः तथा देवेभ्यश्च
> मधुमत्तमोऽत्यंतंस्वादुकारीत्वं कनिक्रदच्छब्दं कुर्वन् सहस्रं पथिभिर्बहुभिर्मार्गैर्याहि । कलशमागच्छ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, you who are for us the best finder (गातुवित्तमः (*gātuvittamaḥ*)) of the way, and for the gods the sweetest (मधुमत्तमः (*madhumattamaḥ*)), the most delicious maker, crying aloud (कनिक्रदत् (*kanikradat*)), go by a thousand, that is many, paths. Come to the vessel.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Soma-deva) अस्मभ्यं (*asmabhyaṃ*)—for us
- गातुवित्तमः (*gātuvittamaḥ*)—the best guide of the way
- देवेभ्यः (*devebhyaḥ*)—for the gods
- मधुमत्तमः (*madhumattamaḥ*)—the most delicious; you such as you are
- कनिक्रदत् (*kanikradat*)—making sound
- सहस्रं पथिभिः (*sahasraṃ pathibhiḥ*)—by many paths
- याहि (*yāhi*)—flow towards the vessel

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, you who are for all of us the best guide of the way and for the gods the most delicious, flow towards the vessel by many paths, making sound.

### Printed p. 52

##### English translation
You who are the best finder of paths for us, the sweet beverage for the gods, come by a thousand paths crying aloud.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पव॑स्व दे॒वनी॑तय॒ इंदो॒ धारा॑भि॒रोज॑सा ।  
> आ कलशं मधुमान्नोऽस्नेम नः सदः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पवस्व । देव्वनीतये । इंदो इति । धाराभिः । ओजसा ।  
> आ । कलशं । मधुमान् । सोम । नः । सदः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंदो सोम देववीतये देवानां भक्षणायौजसा बलेन धाराभिरात्मीयाभिः पवस्व । क्षर । हे सोम मधुमान्मदकररसवांस्त्वं नोऽस्मदीयं
> कलंश द्रोणाभिधानमा सदः ॥ सदेर्लुजि रूपं ॥ आसीद ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indu, Soma, flow with your own streams, with strength, for the feasting of the gods (देववीतये (*devavītaye*)). O Soma, you who are sweet, producing exhilaration, sit in our vessel named Droṇa. सदः (*sadaḥ*) is the form of the root सद (*sada*) with luk; the sense is "sit".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंदो (*iṃdo*)—O Soma-deva
- देवनीतये (*devanītaye*)—for the drinking of the gods
- ओजसा (*ojasā*)—together with your strength
- धाराभिः (*dhārābhiḥ*)—in the form of streams
- पवस्व (*pavasva*)—flow
- सोम (*soma*)—O Soma
- मधुमान् (*madhumān*)—you who are delicious
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- कलशं (*kalaśaṃ*)—in the Droṇa vessel
- आ सदः (*ā sadaḥ*)—sit down

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, flow in streams, together with your strength, for the drinking of the gods. O Soma, you who are delicious, sit in our Droṇa vessel.

##### English translation
Flow, Indu, in your strength with your streams for the banquet of the gods; sit down, Soma, who are sweet-flavoured, in our pitcher.

### Printed p. 53
#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तव॑ द्र॒प्सा उ॑द॒पुत्॒र इंद्रं॒ मदा॑य वावृधुः ।  
> त्वां दे॒वासो॑ अ॒मृता॑य॒ कं प॑पुः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तव । द्रप्साः । उदपुतः । इंद्रं । मदाय । ववृधुः ।  
> त्वां । देवासः । अमृताय । कं । पपुः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उदपुतो वसतीवर्याख्यमुदकं प्रति गच्छंतः । यद्वा । उदकस्य निर्गमयितारः । तव स्वभूता द्रप्सा द्रुतगामिनो रसा मदाय मदार्थमिंद्रं
> वावृधुः । वर्धयंति । ततो देवासो देवा इंद्रादयः कं सुखकरं त्वाममृतायामरणार्थं पपुः । पिबंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

उदपुतः (*udaputaḥ*): going towards the water called Vasatīvarī; or, those who cause water to come out. Your own quick-moving juices (द्रप्साः (*drapsāḥ*)) strengthen Indra for exhilaration. Then the gods, Indra and the rest, drink you, the giver of happiness, for the sake of immortality.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Soma-deva) उदपुतः (*udaputaḥ*)—flowing towards the Vasatīvarī waters and the like
- तव (*tava*)—your
- द्रप्साः (*drapsāḥ*)—juices
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for the joy of Indra
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- वावृधुः (*vāvṛdhuḥ*)—strengthen
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—the gods, Indra and others
- कं (*kaṃ*)—the giver of happiness
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- अमृताय (*amṛtāya*)—for freedom from death
- पपुः (*papuḥ*)—drink

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-deva, your juices, flowing towards the Vasatīvarī waters and the like, strengthen Indra for his joy. The gods, Indra and others, drink you, the giver of happiness, for freedom from death.

##### English translation
Your drops, going to the water, have exalted Indra to exhilaration ; the gods have quaffed you, the deighter‘ for immortality,

### Printed p. 54
#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नः॑ सुतास इंदवः पुना॒ना धा॑वता र॒यिं ।  
> वृ॒ष्टिद्या॑वो रीत्यापः स्व॒र्विदः॑ ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । सुतासः । इंदवः । पुनानाः । धावत । रयिं ।  
> वृष्टिऽद्यावः । रीतिऽपः । स्वःविदः ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सुतासोऽभिनूयमाणा हे इंदवो दीप्ताः पात्रेषु क्षरंतो ना हे
> रीत्यापः यैः पृथिवीं प्रति स्रवणशीला आपः कृताः तादृशा हे सोमाः पुनानः
> सूयमाना यूयं नोऽस्मभ्यं रयिमा धावत । आगमयत । कीदृशाः । वृष्टिद्यावो वृष्टिमभि द्यौर्यैः क्रियते वृष्ट्यभिमुखद्युलोकवंतः स्वर्विदः सर्वस्य
> लंबकाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Somas, pressed and praised, O bright ones, flowing into the vessels, O रीत्यापः (*rītyāpaḥ*), by whom the waters were made to flow towards the earth, O Somas being purified: bring wealth to us. What kind are you? वृष्टिद्यावः (*vṛṣṭidyāvaḥ*): those by whom heaven is made turned towards rain, having the heaven-world facing the rain; स्वर्विदः (*svarvidaḥ*): obtainers of everything.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)—pressed
- इंदवः (*iṃdavaḥ*)—shining
- रीत्यापः (*rītyāpaḥ*)—pouring rain on the earth; O Soma juices
- पुनानाः (*punānāḥ*)—having attained purity
- वृष्टिद्यावः (*vṛṣṭidyāvaḥ*)—filling the sky with rain
- स्वर्विदः (*svarvidaḥ*)—causing all to be obtained; you
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- आ धावत (*ā dhāvata*)—bring and give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma juices, pressed, shining, pouring rain on the earth, purified, filling the sky with rain, causing all to be obtained: bring us wealth.

##### English translation
Bring us wealth, bright Soma juices effused, filtered, filling the sky with rain, shedding water (upon the earth), acquiring all things.

### Printed p. 55
#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सोमः॑ पुना॒न ऊ॒र्मिणाव्यो॒ वारं॒ वि धा॑वति ।  
> अग्रे॑ वा॒चः पव॑मानः॒ कनि॑क्रदत् ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सोमः । पुनानः । ऊर्मिणा । अव्यः । वारं । वि । धावति ।  
> आग्रे । वाचः । पवमानः । कनिक्रदत् ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुनानः पूयमानः सोम ऊर्मिणा स्वीयया धारयाव्योऽवेर्वारं
> वालं पवित्रं वि धावति । विविधं गच्छति । कीदृशः सोमः । पवमानः पूतो वाचः
> स्तोत्रस्याग्रे कनिक्रदत् पुनः पुनः शब्दं कुर्वन्वि धावति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, being purified, runs with his own stream through the sheep's-wool filter (वारं (*vāraṃ*) = the hair, the filter). He is called पवमानः (*pavamānaḥ*), the purified one. Sounding again and again, he runs in front of the voice, that is, of the hymn of praise.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—one who attains purification
- पवमानः (*pavamānaḥ*)—the purifier
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- वाचः (*vācaḥ*)—of the word of praise
- अग्रे (*agre*)—in front
- कनिक्रदत् (*kanikradat*)—making sound
- ऊर्मिणा (*ūrmiṇā*)—with his own stream-form
- अव्यः (*avyaḥ*)—of wool
- वारं (*vāraṃ*)—through the fibres
- वि धावति (*vi dhāvati*)—flows swiftly

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, who is purified and is the purifier, flows swiftly through the fibres of the wool with his own stream-form, sounding in front of the word of praise.

##### English translation
Soma being filtered flows in a stream through the woollen fleece, crying aloud when filtered in advance of the voice (of praise).
#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> धी॒भिर्हि॑न्वंति वा॒जिनं॒ वने॒ क्रीळं॑त॒मत्य॑विं ।  
> अ॒भि त्रि॑पृ॒ष्ठं म॒तयः॒ सम॑स्वरन् ॥ ११ ॥

### Printed p. 56

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> धीभिः । हिन्वंति । वाजिनं । वने । क्रीळंतं । अति अविं ।  
> अभि । त्रिऽपृष्ठं i मतयः । सं । अस्वरन् ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नाजिनं बलवंतं वने वननीये वसतीवर्याख्य उदके क्रीळंतं संक्रीडमानमत्यविं । अविशब्देन तद्रोमकृतं पवित्रमभिधीयते । अतिक्रांतपवित्रं सोममृत्विजो धीभिः स्तुतिभिर्हिन्वंति । वर्धयंति । यद्वा । धीभिः ॥ वर्णलोपश्चांदसः ॥
> धीतिभिरंगुलीभिर्हिन्वंति । प्रेरयंति ॥ हि गतौ वृद्धौ च स्वादिः । किं च त्रिपृष्टं । त्रीणि
> पवित्राणि द्रोणकलशाधवनीयपूतभृदाख्यानि पात्राणि स्पृशतीति त्रीणि सवनानि वा
> स्पृशतीति स तथोक्तः । तं सोमं मतयः स्तुतयोऽभि समस्वरन् । अभितः
> संस्तुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The powerful Soma sports in the water called वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*), and has passed beyond the filter; by अवि (*avi*) is meant the filter made of wool hair. The priests strengthen him with praises (धीभिः (*dhībhiḥ*)). Alternatively, धीभिः (*dhībhiḥ*) means with the fingers (*dhītibhiḥ*, with loss of a letter, a Vedic feature); they urge him on. The root हि (*hi*) is of the Svādi class, in the sense of going and increasing. Further, he is त्रिपृष्ठं (*tripṛṣṭhaṃ*): he touches the three vessels called Droṇakalaśa, Ādhavanīya and Pūtabhṛt, or he touches the three pressings. The praises sing around that Soma on all sides.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वाजिनं (*vājinaṃ*)—the powerful one
- वने (*vane*)—in the Vasatīvarī water
- क्रीळंतं (*krīḷaṃtaṃ*)—the one who sports
- अत्यविं (*atyaviṃ*)—Soma who has crossed the woollen filter
- धीभिः (*dhībhiḥ*)—with praises
- हिन्वंति (*hinvaṃti*)—the priests strengthen
- त्रिपृष्ठं (*tripṛṣṭhaṃ*)—Soma who is in the vessels called Droṇakalaśa, Ādhavanīya and Pūtabhṛt
- मतयः (*matayaḥ*)—praises
- अभि समस्वरन् (*abhi samasvaran*)—praise on all sides

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The priests strengthen with praises Soma, who is powerful, sports in the Vasatīvarī water and has crossed the woollen filter. The praises extol on all sides Soma who is in the vessels called Droṇakalaśa, Ādhavanīya and Pūtabhṛt.

##### English translation
They send forth with their fingers the powerful Soma sporting in the water, passing through the fleece; praises celebrate him abiding in the hree receptacles.
#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अस॑र्जि क॒लशा॑ अ॒भि मि॒ळ्ळे सप्ति॒र्न वा॑ज॒युः ।  
> पु॒ना॒नो वाचं॑ ज॒नय॑न्नसिष्यदत् ॥ १२ ॥

### Printed p. 57

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> असर्जि । कलशान् । अभि । माळ्ही । सप्तिः । न । वाजयुः ।  
> प्रनानः । वाचं । जनयन् । असिस्त्यदत् ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वाजयुर्यजमानानामन्नमिच्छन् स सोमः कलशानभिलक्ष्य कलशेष्वसर्जि ।
> सृज्यते । तत्र दृष्टांतः । सप्तिर्न यथाश्वो मिळ्ळे ! संग्रामनामैतत् । संग्रामे
> सृज्यते तद्वत् । ततः पुनानः पूयमानः सोमो वाचं शब्दं जनयन्नुत्पादयन्नसिष्यदत् । पात्रेषु स्यंदते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, desiring food for the sacrificers (वाजयुः (*vājayuḥ*)), is let loose into the pitchers. The illustration: as a horse is let loose in मिळ्ळे (*miḷḷe*), a name for battle, so is he. Then, being purified, Soma produces a sound and flows into the vessels.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सप्तिः (*saptiḥ*)—a horse
- मिळ्ळे न (*miḷḷe na*)—as it is let loose for battle
- वाजयुः (*vājayuḥ*)—Soma, wishing to provide food for the sacrificer
- कलशान् अभि (*kalaśān abhi*)—into the pitcher-vessels
- असर्जि (*asarji*)—is let loose
- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—Soma who has been purified
- वाचं (*vācaṃ*)—sound
- जनयन् (*janayan*)—producing
- असिष्यदत् (*asiṣyadat*)—enters the vessels

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As a horse is let loose for battle, Soma, wishing to provide food for the sacrificer, is let loose into the pitcher-vessels. Soma, having been purified, enters the vessels producing a sound.

##### English translation
Desiring (to give the worshipper) food he has been let loose into the pitchers like a charger in battle ; being filtered, raising a cry he flows (into the vessels)
#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पव॑ते हर्य॒ॆतो हरि॒रति॒ ह्वरां॑सि॒ रंह्या॑ ।  
> अभ्यर्षन्‌न्नू नृत्यभ्यो वीरवद्यशः ॥ १३ ॥

### Printed p. 58

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पवते । हर्यतः । हरिः । अति । हृरांसि । रंह्या ।  
> अभिर् अर्षन् । स्तोतृभ्यः । वीरवत् । यशः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हर्यतः स्पृहणीयो हरिर्हरितवर्णः सोमो रंह्मा ॥ तृतीयाया
> आकारः ॥ साधुवेगेन ह्वरांसि कुटिलान्यनृजूनि पवित्राण्यति पवते । अतीत्य गच्छति ।
> किं कुर्वन् । स्तोतृभ्यो वीरवत् पुत्रयुक्तं यशोभ्यर्षन्नभिगमयन ॥ पवते

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, desirable (हर्यतः (*haryataḥ*)) and green-tinted (हरिः (*hariḥ*)), flows with good speed (रंह्या (*raṃhyā*), the instrumental ending in ā) through the crooked, that is, not straight, filters, passing over them. What does he do? He brings to the worshippers fame together with sons.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हरतः (*harataḥ*)—one of attractive form
- हरिः (*hariḥ*)—the green-coloured Soma-god
- स्तोतृभ्यः (*stotṛbhyaḥ*)—to the praisers
- वीरवत् (*vīravat*)—accompanied by heroic sons and the like
- यशः (*yaśaḥ*)—fame
- अभ्यर्षन् (*abhyarṣan*)—bestowing
- रंह्या (*raṃhyā*)—with speed
- ह्वरांसि (*hvarāṃsi*)—to the crooked vessels
- अति पवते (*ati pavate*)—flows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Soma-god, of attractive form and green colour, flows swiftly into the crooked vessels, bestowing on the praisers fame accompanied by heroic sons and the like.

##### English translation
The delightful green-tinted (juice) flows with rapidity into the crooked (pitchers); bringing the worshippers food and male offspring.
**NOTE**
#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒या प॑वस्व देव॒युर्मधो॒धारा॑ अस्यक्षत ।  
> रेभ॑स्प॒वित्रं॒ पर्ये॑षि वि॒श्वतः॑ ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अया । पवस्व । देवयुति । मधोः । धाराः । अस्पृशत ।  
> रेभन् । पवित्रं । परि । एषि । विश्वतः । १४ ॥

### Printed p. 59

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम देवयुर्देवानामयमानस्त्वमयानया धारया पवस्व ।
> क्षर । ततो मधोर्मदकरस्य तव धारा आत्मीया असृक्षत । स्यज्यते । अनंतरं रेभञ्छब्दायमानः पवित्रं विश्वतः पर्येषि । सर्वतः परिगच्छसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, desiring the gods (देवयुः (*devayuḥ*)), flow with this stream. Then your streams, of intoxicating sweetness, are let loose. Afterwards, sounding, you go round the filter on all sides.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Soma-god) देवयुः (*devayuḥ*)—you who long for the gods
- अया (*ayā*)—with this stream-form
- पवस्व (*pavasva*)—flow
- मधोः (*madhoḥ*)—of you, the exhilarating one
- धाराः (*dhārāḥ*)—streams
- असृक्षत (*asṛkṣata*)—are let loose
- रेभन् (*rebhan*)—making sound
- पवित्रं (*pavitraṃ*)—to the filter
- विश्वतः (*viśvataḥ*)—from all sides
- पर्येषि (*paryeṣi*)—come and join

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma-god, you who desire to join the gods, flow with this stream-form. Your streams, which cause exhilaration, flow. Sounding, come from all sides to the filter.

##### English translation
Devouted to the gods flow with that (stream); (your) streams of exhilarating liquor are let loose ; with a roar you pass through the filter in all directions.
## Sūkta 107 (Maṇḍala 9)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> परीत इति षड्विंशत्यचं चतुर्थं सूक्तं । त्वं सोमासि । ऋ. ९.६७ । इत्यत्रोक्ता भरद्वाजकश्यपाद्याः सप्तर्षयः । आद्या बृहती द्वितीया सतोबृहती ।
> अयमेकः प्रगाथः । परि सुवान इत्येषा तृतीयैकविंशत्यक्षरा भुरिग्विराड् द्विपदा ।
> नृभिर्यॆमान इति षोडशिविंशत्यक्षरा द्विपदा विराट् । चतुर्थ्यादिद्वौ प्रगाथौ ।
> दशम्यादित्रयः सप्तदश्यादिपंच प्रगाथाः । पवमानः सोमो देवता । तथा
> चानुक्रम्यते । परीतः षड्विंशतिः सप्तर्षयः प्रागाथं तृतीयाषोळश्या द्विपदे
> अष्टम्याद्ये बृहत्याविति ॥ गतः सूक्तविनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

परीत (*parīta*) is the fourth sūkta, of twenty-six verses. The seers are the seven seers, Bharadvāja, Kaśyapa and others, as stated at त्वं सोमासि (*tvaṃ somāsi*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 9.67). The first verse is Bṛhatī, the second Satobṛhatī; this pair is one Pragātha. The third, परि सुवान (*pari suvāna*), of twenty-one syllables, is Bhurigvirāḍ Dvipadā. The sixteenth, नृभिर्यॆमान (*nṛbhiryèmāna*), of twenty syllables, is Dvipadā Virāṭ. The fourth and following form two Pragāthas; the tenth and following three, and the seventeenth and following five, are Pragāthas. The deity is Pavamāna Soma. The Anukramaṇī says: परीतः षड्विंशतिः सप्तर्षयः प्रागाथं तृतीयाषोळश्या द्विपदे अष्टम्याद्ये बृहत्याविति ॥ (*parītaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ saptarṣayaḥ prāgāthaṃ tṛtīyāṣoḷaśyā dvipade aṣṭamyādye bṛhatyāviti ‖*) The use of the sūkta has been stated.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Translation: This sūkta परीतः (*parītaḥ*) is the fourth sūkta in the seventh Anuvāka. It has twenty-six Ṛks; the preceding statement was made at sūkta 66 of this Maṇḍala, त्वं सोमासि (*tvaṃ somāsi*).

### Printed p. 60
The seven seers, Bharadvāja, Kaśyapa and the others, are the seers of this sūkta too. Pavamāna Soma is the deity. The first Ṛk of this sūkta is in the Bṛhatī metre and the second in the Satobṛhatī metre. Thus a Ṛk of the Bṛhatī metre together with the Ṛk of Satobṛhatī metre that follows it is called a Pragātha. The third Ṛk, परि सुवानः (*pari suvānaḥ*), is in the metre called Bhurigvirāṭ Dvipadā of twenty-one syllables; the sixteenth Ṛk, नैभिर्यॆमानः (*naibhiryèmānaḥ*), is in the metre called Dvipadā of sixteen syllables. From the fourth Ṛk onwards there are two Pragāthas. Three Ṛks from the tenth and five Ṛks from the seventeenth are Pragāthas. In the Anukramaṇikā it is stated: परीतः षड्विंशतिः सप्तर्षयः प्रागाथं तृतीयषोळश्या द्विपदे अष्टमाद्ये बृहत् (*parītaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ saptarṣayaḥ prāgāthaṃ tṛtīyaṣoḷaśyā dvipade aṣṭamādye bṛhat*). The use of the sūkta was stated earlier.
**NOTE**
Maṇḍala 9; Anuvāka 7; Sūkta 107. Aṣṭaka 7; Adhyāya 5; Vargas 12, 13, 14, 15, 16. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 26.
Seers: the seven seers (Bharadvāja Bārhaspatya, Kaśyapa Mārīca, Gotama Rāhūgaṇa, Atri Bhauma, Viśvāmitra Gāthina, Jamadagni Bhārgava, Vasiṣṭha Maitrāvaruṇi).
Deity: Pavamāna Soma.
Metre: 1, 4, 6, 8–10, 12, 14, 17, 19, 21, 23, 25 Bṛhatī; 2, 5, 7, 11, 13, 15, 18, 20, 22, 24, 26 Satobṛhatī; 3, 16 Dvipadā.
#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> परी॒तो षिं॑चता सु॒तं सोमो॒ य उ॑त्त॒मं ह॒विः ।  
> द॒ध॒न्वा यो नर्यो॑ अ॒प्स्व१॒ं॑१तरा सु॒षाव॒ सोम॒मुद्रि॑भिः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> परि । इतः । सिंचत । सुतं । सोमः । यः । उत्तमं । हविः ।  
> दधन्वान् । यः । नर्यः । अप्सु । अंतः । आ । सुसाव । सोमं । अद्रिभिः ॥ १

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋत्विजः सुतमभिषुतं सोममित अस्मात्कर्मण ऊर्ध्वं अथवास्मात्
> देशादूर्ध्वं परि षिंचत वसतीवरीभिः ॥ इतो षिंचतेत.त. संहितायां छंदसीति

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O priests, pour round, away from this rite (or, upward from this place), the pressed Soma, with the Vasatīvarī waters. ()

### Printed p. 61

> रोरुत्वं । आदेशप्रत्यययोरिति षत्वं ॥ यः सोमो देवानामुत्तमं प्रशस्यं
> हविर्भवति आ अपि च नर्यो मनुष्यहितो यश्च सोमोऽप्सु वसतीवरीष्टंतरिक्षे
> नांतर्दधन्वान्गच्छन्भवति तं सोममदिर्भिग्रावभिरध्वर्युः सुषाव । अभिषुतं
> चकार । तं परि षिंचतेति समन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Adhvaryu pressed that Soma with the pressing stones. Soma is the best, most praiseworthy oblation of the gods. He is beneficial to men. He moves within the waters called वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) in the firmament. Sāyaṇa's construction is: sprinkle (परि षिंचत (*pari ṣiṃcata*)) that Soma, pressed by the Adhvaryu with the stones. The opening part of the block concerns the phonetic changes of षिंचत (*ṣiṃcata*) and is left to the grammar.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (Oh priests) यः सोमः (*yaḥ somaḥ*)—which Soma
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—for the gods
- उत्तमं (*uttamaṃ*)—the best, praiseworthy
- हवी (*havī*)—is an oblation
- आ (*ā*)—and
- नर्यः (*naryaḥ*)—is beneficial to men
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Soma
- अप्सु (*apsu*)—of the वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) and other waters
- अंतः (*aṃtaḥ*)—in the middle
- दधन्वान् (*dadhanvān*)—moves
- (यं (*yaṃ*)—which Soma)
- अद्रिभिः (*adribhiḥ*)—with the pressing stones
- सुषाव (*suṣāva*)—the Adhvaryu has pressed
- सुतं (*sutaṃ*)—pressed
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—Soma
- इतः (*itaḥ*)—from this place
- परि षिंचत (*pari ṣiṃcata*)—sprinkle (make it flow)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Oh priests, the Soma that is the best, praiseworthy oblation for the gods and beneficial to men, that moves in the midst of the वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) and other waters, and that the Adhvaryu has pressed with the pressing stones: make that pressed Soma flow from this place.

##### English translation
Sprinkle from hence the effused Soma-juice which is the best oblation (to the gods), and which, good for man, is going into the midst of the firmament ; (the priest) has expressed the Soma with the stones.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू॒नं पु॑ना॒नोऽवि॑भिः॒ परि॑ स्र॒वाद॑ब्धः सुर॒भंत॑रः ।  
> सु॒ते चि॑त्वा॒प्सु म॑दामो॒ अंध॑सा श्री॒णंतो॒ गोभि॒रुत्त॑रं॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नूनं । पुनानः । अविभिः । परि । स्रव । अदब्धः । सुरभिंतरः ।  
> सुते । चित् । त्वा । अप्ऽसु । मदामः । अंधसा । श्रीणंतः । गोभिः । उत्तरं । २ ॥

### Printed p. 62

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम अदब्धः कैश्चिदप्यहिंसितः सुरभंतरोऽत्यंतं सुगंधिस्त्वं नूनमिदानीं पुनानः पूयमानः सन्नविभिरविवालकृतैः पवित्रैस्तेभ्यः परि स्रव। परितः क्षर।
> सुते चिदभिषुते सत्यंधसा सक्तुलक्षणेनान्नेन गोभिर्गोविकारैः क्षीरैः क्षीरादिभिश्रीणंतो मिश्रयंतो वयमुत्तरमुद्गततरमप्पु वसतीवरीषु संस्थितं त्वां
> मदामः । मंदामहे । स्तुमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Oh Soma, you are unharmed by anyone and exceedingly fragrant. Now, being purified through the sheep-wool filters, flow all around. Now that you are pressed, we praise you, who stand eminent in the waters called वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*). We mix you with food in the form of barley meal and with the products of cows, milk and the like. मदामः (*madāmaḥ*) is explained as मंदामहे (*maṃdāmahe*), स्तुमः (*stumaḥ*) (we praise).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (Oh Soma) अदब्धः (*adabdhaḥ*)—not harmed by anyone
- सुरभिंतरः (*surabhiṃtaraḥ*)—exceedingly fragrant, you who are such
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—having become purified
- अविभिः (*avibhiḥ*)—through the ten-fold filter
- परि स्रव (*pari srava*)—flow outward
- सुते चित् (*sute cit*)—at the time you are pressed
- अप्पु (*appu*)—in the वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) waters
- उत्तरं (*uttaraṃ*)—having attained a high place
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- अंधसा (*aṃdhasā*)—with the oblation
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—with milk and the like
- श्रीणंतः (*śrīṇaṃtaḥ*)—mixing
- मदामः (*madāmaḥ*)—we praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Oh Soma, you who are unharmed by anyone and exceedingly fragrant, now that you have become purified, flow outward through the ten-fold filter. At the time you were pressed you attained a high place in the वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) waters. We praise you, mixing you with the oblation and with milk and the like.

##### English translation
(Soma) who are unassailable, who are most fragrant, flow around now being purified by the fleece ; being effused we praise you eminent in the waters mixing you with food and the (products of the) cows.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पठ॑ सुवा॒नश्चक्ष॑से देव॒माद॑नः॒ क्रतु॒रिंदु॑र्विचक्ष॒णः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> हरि । सुवानः । चक्षसे । देव्यमादनः ; क्रतुः । इंदुः । विचक्षणः ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 63

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुवानोऽभिषूयमाणः सोमश्चक्षसे सर्वेषां दर्शनाय परि स्रवति ।
> कीदृशः । देवमादनो देवानां तर्पयिता क्रतुः कर्ता इंदुः पात्रेषु क्षरणशीलो दीपो ना विचक्षणः सर्वस्य विद्रष्टा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, being pressed, flows all around so that all may see him. What kind is he? He is the exhilarator of the gods, the agent (क्रतुः (*kratuḥ*)), and इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*), who is given to flowing into the vessels. He is like a lamp, the observer of all (विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवमादनः (*devamādanaḥ*)—the one who gratifies the gods
- क्रतुः (*kratuḥ*)—the bearer of the rite
- विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*)—the one of subtle vision
- इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*)—Soma
- सुवानः (*suvānaḥ*)—having been pressed
- चक्षसे (*cakṣase*)—to be seen by all
- परि (*pari*) (स्रवते (*sravate*))—flows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, who gratifies the gods, bears the rite and has subtle vision, flows, having been pressed, so that all may see him.

##### English translation
Being effused the Indu flows so as to be seen (of all) the exhilarator of the gods, the agent (in holy acts), the observer of all.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पु॒ना॒नः सो॑म॒ धार॑या॒पो वसा॑नो अर्षसि ।  
> आ र॑त्न॒धा योनि॑मृ॒तस्य॑ सीद॒स्युत्सो॑ देव हिर॒ण्मयः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रनानः । सोम । धारया ' अपः ! वसानः । अर्षसि ।  
> आ । रत्नधाः । योनिं । ऋतस्य । सीदसि । उत्सः । देव । हिरण्ययः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम पुनानः पूयमानस्त्वमप उदकानि वसतीवर्याख्यानि वसानॆ
> आच्छादयन् धारयार्षसि । पवित्रं गच्छसि । ततो रत्नधा रत्नानां दाता त्वमृतस्य

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Oh Soma, being purified, you clothe yourself in the waters called वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) and flow in a stream; that is, you go to the filter. Then, as giver of jewels, you sit on the seat, the place of the sacrifice that is true (ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)). (The block continues on the next page.)

### Printed p. 64

> सत्यभूतस्य यज्ञस्य योनिं स्थानमा सीदसि । हे देव द्योतमान सोम उत्सः
> प्रस्यंदनशीलस्त्वं हिरण्ययो हिरण्मयो हिरण्यस्योत्पत्तिस्थानं खलु । यद्वा ।
> देवानां हितरमणीयो भवसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Oh shining Soma, you who are given to flowing (उत्सः (*utsaḥ*)) are indeed हिरण्ययः (*hiraṇyayaḥ*), golden, the place where gold arises. Or else: you become pleasing, dear to the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—oh Soma
- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—you who are being purified
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—the वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) and other waters
- वसानः (*vasānaḥ*)—covering
- धारया (*dhārayā*)—in your stream-form
- अर्षसि (*arṣasi*)—you go to the ten-fold filter
- रत्नधाः (*ratnadhāḥ*)—you who bestow wealth
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sacrifice that is of the nature of truth
- योनिं (*yoniṃ*)—the place, having occupied it
- आ सीदसि (*ā sīdasi*)—you sit
- देव (*deva*)—oh shining Soma
- उत्सः (*utsaḥ*)—you who are given to flowing
- हिरण्ययः (*hiraṇyayaḥ*)—you are beneficial and attractive to the gods **NOTE**

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Oh Soma, being purified, you cover the वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) and other waters and in your stream-form go to the ten-fold filter. As bestower of wealth you occupy the place of the sacrifice, which is of the nature of truth, and sit there. Oh shining Soma, given to flowing, you are beneficial and attractive to the gods.

##### English translation
Undergoing purification you flow Soma in a stream clothed in the (consecrated) waters; the giver of wealth you sit on the place of sacrifice, divine (Soma), who flow forth, who are the origin of gold.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दु॒हा॒न ऊध॑र्दि॒व्यं मधु॑ प्रि॒यं प्र॒त्नं स॒धस्थ॒मास॑दत् ।  
> आ॒पृच्छ्यं॑ ध॒रुणं॑ वा॒ज्य॑र्षति॒ नृभि॑र्दू॒तो वि॑चक्ष॒णः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दुहानः । ऊधः । दिव्यं । मधु । प्रियं । प्रत्नं । सध्वस्थं । असदत् ।  
> आपृच्छं । धरुणं । वाजी । अर्षति । नृभिः । धूतः । विचक्षणः ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 65

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मधु मदकरं प्रियं प्रीणनकारि दिव्यं दिवि भवमूधः सोमवल्लीलक्षणं
> महानः सोमः प्रत्नं पुरातनं सथस्थं ! सह तिष्ठंत्यत्रेति सथस्थं स्थानं । अंतरिक्षमासदत् । आसीदति ॥ सदेर्लुणु रूपं ॥ तत आपृच्छ्यं कर्मण्यापृष्टव्यं धरुणं
> कर्मणो धारयितारं यजमानं वाज्यन्नवान्नोमोऽर्षति ! तस्मा अन्नं दातुमभिगच्छति । कीदृशः । नृभिः कर्मनेतृभिर्युत्विग्निर्धूतोऽदाभ्यग्रहे परिशोधितः ।
> तैरेनं चतुराधूनोति पंचकृत्वः सप्तकृत्तो वेत्यापस्तंबेन सूत्रितं । विचक्षणः
> सर्वस्य विद्रष्टा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma milks the celestial udder, that is, the Soma plant, which is intoxicating (मधु (*madhu*)) and pleasing. He sits in the ancient सथस्थं (*sathasthaṃ*) (the place where they stand together), the firmament; आसदत् (*āsadat*) is a form of the root *sad*. Then, rich in food (वाजी (*vājī*)), he goes to the worshipper, the sustainer of the rite, who is to be consulted in the rite (आपृच्छ्यं (*āpṛcchyaṃ*)), in order to give him food. He is cleansed (धूतः (*dhūtaḥ*)) by the priests, the leaders of the rite, in the *adābhya* cup. Āpastamba's sūtra gives this: they shake him four times, five times or seven times. He is the observer of all.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मधु (*madhu*)—tasty
- प्रियं (*priyaṃ*)—agreeable
- दिव्यं (*divyaṃ*)—born in the celestial world
- ऊधः (*ūdhaḥ*)—the udder (the Soma plant)
- दुहानः (*duhānaḥ*)—the Soma who milks
- सदस्यं (*sadasyaṃ*)—the firmament **NOTE**
- आसदत् (*āsadat*)—takes refuge in
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—by the priests
- धूतः (*dhūtaḥ*)—cleansed
- विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*)—of subtle vision
- वाजी (*vājī*)—strong Soma
- आपृच्छ्यं (*āpṛcchyaṃ*)—praiseworthy
- धरुणं (*dharuṇaṃ*)—to the worshipper who bears the rite
- अर्षति (*arṣati*)—goes and joins

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, who milks the tasty, agreeable udder born in the celestial world (the Soma plant), takes refuge in the firmament. Cleansed by the priests, of subtle vision and strong, Soma goes and joins the praiseworthy worshipper who bears the rite.

##### English translation
Milking the exhilarating agreeable celestial udder (the Soma) sits down on its ancient place of aggregation ( the firmament ); food-giving, cleansed by the priests, observant it flows to the commendable worshipper.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्रस॒न्॒नः सो॑म॒ जागृ॑पि॒रव्यो॒ वारे॒ परि॑ प्रि॒यः ।  
> त्वं विप्रो॑ अभ॒वोऽगि॑रस्तमो॒ मध्वा॑ य॒ज्ञं मि॑मिक्ष नः ॥  
> xxv 5  
> xxv 5

### Printed p. 66

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रनानः । सोम । जागृविः । अव्यः । वारे । परि । प्रियः ।  
> त्वं । विप्रः । अभवः । अंगिरःतमः । मध्वा । यज्ञं । मिमिक्ष्य । नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम जागृविर्जागरणशीलः प्रियः प्रीणयिता त्वं पुनानः पूयमानः
> सन्नव्योऽवेर्वारे वाले पवित्रे परि क्षरसि । किंच विप्रो मेधावी त्वमंगिरस्तमोऽंगिरसां वरिष्ठः पित्थूणां नेताभवः । भवसि । स त्वं नोऽस्मदीयं यज्ञं
> मध्वा मधुनात्मीयेन रसेन मिमिक्ष्य । सिंच ॥ मिहेः सेचनार्थस्य सनि रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Oh Soma, vigilant (जागृविः (*jāgṛviḥ*)) and dear (प्रियः (*priyaḥ*)), being purified you flow around in the hair (वारे (*vāre*)) of the sheep, the filter. Moreover, you, the wise (विप्रः (*vipraḥ*)), have become the best of the Aṅgirases, the leader of the fathers. Such as you are, sprinkle (मिमिक्ष्य (*mimikṣya*)) our sacrifice with your own sweet juice. The form is from the desiderative of the root *mih*, in the sense of sprinkling.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—oh Soma
- जागृविः (*jāgṛviḥ*)—vigilant
- प्रियः (*priyaḥ*)—beneficial
- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—purified, you who are such
- अव्यः (*avyaḥ*)—of wool
- वारे (*vāre*)—through the fibres
- परि (*pari*)—you flow
- विप्रः (*vipraḥ*)—wise
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- अंगिरस्तमः (*aṃgirastamaḥ*)—the best of all the Aṅgirases
- अभवः (*abhavaḥ*)—you have become
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—sacrifice
- मध्वा (*madhvā*)—with sweet Soma juice
- मिमिक्षु (*mimikṣu*)—sprinkle **NOTE**

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Oh Soma, you who are vigilant, beneficial and purified, flow through the fibres of wool. You, the wise, have become the best among all the Aṅgirases. Sprinkle our sacrifice with sweet Soma juice.

##### English translation
Soma, who are purified, vigilant, agreeable (you flow ) through the woolen fleece ; you are intelligent, the chief of the Angirasas, squinkle our oblation with your exhilarating juice.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सोमो॑ मी॒ढ्वान्न॑वते गातु॒वित्त॑म॒ ऋषि॒र्विप्रो॑ विचक्ष॒णः ।  
> त्वं क॒विर॑भवो देव॒वीत॑म॒ आ सूर्यं॑ रोहयो दि॒वि ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 67

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सोमः । मीढ्वान् । पवते ! गातुवित् 5तमः । यषिः । विप्रः । विचक्षणः ।  
> त्वं । कविः । अभवः । देव्यवीतमः । आ । सूर्यं । रोहयः । दिवि ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मीढ्वान् कामानां सेक्ता सोमः पवते । क्षरति । कीदृशः । गातुवित्तमोऽत्यंतं मार्गस्य लंभक ऋषिः सर्वस्य प्रदर्शको विप्रो मेधावी विचक्षणो
> विद्रष्टा । किंच कविः क्रांतप्रज्ञस्त्वं देववीतमोऽत्यंतं देवकामोऽभवः । भवसि !
> अपि च दिवि द्युलोके सूर्यमा रोहयः । प्रादुर्भावयसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, the showerer of desires, flows (पवते (*pavate*) means "trickles"). He is गातुवित्तमः (*gātuvittamaḥ*), the supreme finder of the path, ऋषिः (*ṛṣiḥ*), the one who shows everything, विप्रः (*vipraḥ*), the wise, and विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*), the far-seeing. Further, Soma is addressed as कविः (*kaviḥ*), one of advanced understanding. He has become supremely desirous of the gods (अभवः (*abhavaḥ*) means "you are"). And in heaven, the world of Dyu, he makes the sun mount, that is, brings it forth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- गातुवित्तमः (*gātuvittamaḥ*)—one who knows the best path
- ऋषिः (*ṛṣiḥ*)—one of divine vision
- विप्रः (*vipraḥ*)—the wise
- विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*)—one of subtle vision
- मीढ्वान् (*mīḍhvān*)—the showerer of desires
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- पवते (*pavate*)—flows (O Soma)
- कविः (*kaviḥ*)—of advanced understanding
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- देववीतमः (*devavītamaḥ*)—having become desirous of the gods
- अभवः (*abhavaḥ*)—you have been
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the world of Dyu
- सूर‍्यं (*sūryaṃ*)—the sun
- आ रोहयः (*ā rohayaḥ*)—you have made to mount

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, who knows the best path, is of divine vision, wise, of subtle vision and showers desires, flows. O Lord Soma, you of advanced understanding have become desirous of the gods. You have established the sun in the world of Dyu.

##### English translation
Soma flows forth the showerer of benefits, the best of path-finders, a Rishi, a sage, all-discriminating: you are a seer, most devoted to the gods; you have made the sun to mount the sky.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सोम॑ उषुवा॒णः सो॒तृभि॒रधि॒ ष्णुभि॒रवी॑नां ।  
> अश्व॑येव ह॒रिता॑ याति॒ धार॑या मं॒द्रया॑ याति॒ धार॑या ॥

### Printed p. 68

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सोमः । ऊं इति । सुवानः । सोतृभिः । अधि । स्नुभिः । अवीनां ।  
> अश्वया इव । हरिता । याति । धारया । मंद्रया । याति । धारया ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सोतृभिरभिषुण्वद्भिर्यृत्विग्निः सुवानोऽभिषूयमाणः सोमोऽवीनां
> स्नुभिः । मांसृत्सूनामुपसंख्यानं । पा. ६-१-६३-१ । इति सानुशब्दस्य स्नुभावः ॥
> समुच्छ्रितैर्वालैः पवित्रैरधि याति । अधिकं गच्छति । उ इति प्रसिद्धौ । अश्वयेव
> बडवयेव हरिता हरितवर्णया धारया याति । मंद्रया मदकारिण्या धारया
> द्रोणकलशमभिगच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, pressed (सुवानः (*suvānaḥ*)) by the pressing priests (सोतृभिः (*sotṛbhiḥ*)), flows over the स्नुभिः (*snubhiḥ*) of the अवीनां (*avīnāṃ*), that is, over the raised woollen filaments, the strainers; *adhi yāti* means he goes beyond them. उ (*u*) is the well-known particle. अश्वयेव (*aśvayeva*) means "like a mare". He goes with a green-tinted stream, and with an exhilarating stream he goes to the Droṇa vessel.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोतृभिः (*sotṛbhiḥ*)—by the priests who press
- सुवानः (*suvānaḥ*)—being pressed
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- अवीनां (*avīnāṃ*)—of the ten-fold strainers (daśāpavitra)
- स्नुभिः (*snubhiḥ*)—through the fibres
- *adhi yāti*—flows especially
- अश्वयेव (*aśvayeva*)—as if going mounted on a horse
- हरिता (*haritā*)—green-hued
- धारया (*dhārayā*)—with a stream
- याति (*yāti*)—flows
- मंद्रया (*maṃdrayā*)—agreeable
- धारया (*dhārayā*)—with a stream he rushes forward

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Lord Soma, pressed by the priests engaged in the pressing rite, flows especially through the fibres of the ten-fold strainer. As if going mounted on a horse, he flows with a green-hued stream. He rushes forward with an agreeable stream.

##### English translation
Effused by the effusers Soma is poured out upon the elevated fleeces; and proceeds with its green-tinted stream, as on a mare, proceeds with its exhilarating stream.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒नू॒पे गोमा॒न्नोभि॑रक्षाः॒ सोमो॑ दु॒ग्धाभि॑रक्षाः ।  
> स॒मु॒द्रं न सं॒वर॑णान्यग्मन्मं॒दी मदा॑य तोशते ॥ ९ ॥

### Printed p. 69

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अनूपे । गोऽमान् । गोभिः । अक्षारिति । सोमः । दुग्धाभिः । अक्षारिति ।  
> समुद्रं । न । संवरणानि । अग्मन् । मंदी । मदाय । तोशते ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> गोमान्नोयुक्तः सोमोऽनूपे निम्मे देशे कलशे गोभिर्गॊ विकारैः
> क्षीरादिभिः सहाक्षाः । क्षरति । तदेवोच्यते । स सोम आत्मनो मिश्रणार्थं
> दुग्धाभिर्गॊभिः सहाक्षाः । क्षरति ॥ क्षरतेर्लुजु रूपं ॥ किंच समुद्रं न यथा
> समुद्रमुदकानि गच्छंति तद्वत्संवरणानि संभजनीयानि रसरूपाण्यन्नानि द्रोणकलशमग्मन् । गच्छंति ॥ गमर्लुजु चेर्लुजु रूपं ॥ किंच मंदी मदकरः सोमो
> मदाय मदार्थं तोशते । हन्यते । अभिषूयते । तोशतिर्वधकर्मा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, गोमान् (*gomān*), joined with the cow-products, flows into the vessel in the low-lying place (अनूपे (*anūpe*) = निम्मे देशे (*nimme deśe*)) together with milk and the like (विकारैः क्षीरादिभिः (*vikāraiḥ kṣīrādibhiḥ*)). The same is said again: for mixing himself, Soma flows with the milked cows. अक्षाः (*akṣāḥ*) is a luk-form of the root क्षर (*kṣara*). Further, as waters go to the ocean, so the संवरणानि (*saṃvaraṇāni*), the sap-form foods to be shared, go to the Droṇa vessel (अग्मन् (*agman*) = "they go", a luk-form of *gam*). And Soma, the exhilarating one (मंदी (*maṃdī*)), is तोशते (*tośate*), struck, that is pressed, for exhilaration; तोशति (*tośati*) is a verb of killing.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- गोमान् (*gomān*)—Soma joined with milk
- अनूपे (*anūpe*)—to the vessel in your region **NOTE**
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—with milk and the like
- अक्षाः (*akṣāḥ*)—flows
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- दुग्धाभिः (*dugdhābhiḥ*)—with milk and the like
- अक्षाः (*akṣāḥ*)—flows
- समुद्रं न (*samudraṃ na*)—as waters go and join the ocean
- संवरणानि (*saṃvaraṇāni*)—his venerable juices
- अग्मन् (*agman*)—go and join the Droṇa vessel
- मंदी (*maṃdī*)—Soma, the gladdener
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for the gladness of the gods
- तोशते (*tośate*)—is pressed

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, joined with milk, goes and joins the vessel in your region together with milk and the like. He flows mixed with milk and the like. As waters go and join the ocean, his venerable juices go and join the Droṇa vessel. Soma, the gladdener, is pressed for the gladness of the gods.

##### English translation
Soma combined with the produce of the cows flows with the cows into the pitcher, he flows with the milked cows; his enjoyable juices go (to the pitcher as waters) to the ocean, the exhilarating (Soma) is expressed for the exhilaration (of the gods).

### Printed p. 70

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ सो॑म सुवा॒नो अद्रि॑भिस्ति॒रो वारा॑ण्य॒व्यया॑ ।  
> जनो॒ न पु॒रि च॒न्वो॑र्विश॒द्धरिः॒ सदो॒ वने॑षु दधिषे ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । सोम । सुवानः । अद्रिभिः । तिरः । नाराणि । अव्यया ।  
> जनः । न । पुरि । चम्योः । विशत् । हरिः । सदः । वनेषु । दधिषे ॥ १० ॥

**NOTE**

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम अद्रिभिग्रावभिः सुवानोऽभिषूयमाणस्त्वमव्ययाविमयानि
> वाराणि वालानि पवित्राणि तिरस्कुर्वन् व्यवधायकानि कुर्वाणः सन्ना पवस इति शेषः ।
> हरिर्हरितवर्णः स सोमश्चन्वोरधिषवणफलकयोरुपिरि स्थिते कलशे विशत् । प्रविशति
> तत्र दृष्टांतः । जनो न यथा जनः पुरि पुरे प्रविशति । स त्वं वनेषु काष्ठनिर्मितेषु पात्रेषु सदः स्थानं दधिषे । करोषि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, pressed (सुवानः (*suvānaḥ*)) by the stones (अद्रिभिः (*adribhiḥ*), the pressing stones), you flow, the verb पवसे (*pavase*) being understood, making the woollen strainer-hairs (अव्यया (*avyayā*) = *avimayāni*, वाराणि (*vārāṇi*) = वालानि (*vālāni*)) pass across (तिरः (*tiraḥ*)), that is, making them intervene. The green-tinted (हरिः (*hariḥ*)) Soma enters (विशत् (*viśat*)) the vessel set on the two *canvoḥ*, the pressing boards. The illustration: as a man (जनः (*janaḥ*)) enters a city (पुरि (*puri*)). You then make a seat (सदः (*sadaḥ*)) in the vessels made of wood (वनेषु (*vaneṣu*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O Lord Soma
- अद्रिभिः (*adribhiḥ*)—by the pressing stones
- सुवानः (*suvānaḥ*)—you who are pressed
- अव्यया (*avyayā*)—of wool
- वाराणि (*vārāṇi*)—the ten-fold strainer with its fibres
- तिरः (*tiraḥ*)—crossing over
- आ (पवसे) (*ā (pavase)*)—you flow
- पुरि (*puri*)—into a town
- जनः न (*janaḥ na*)—as a townsman enters
- हरिः (*hariḥ*)—the green-hued Soma
- चन्नोः (*cannoḥ*)—into the vessel on the boards **NOTE**
- विशत् (*viśat*)—enters
- वनेषु (*vaneṣu*)—in the vessels
- सदः (*sadaḥ*)—a seat
- दधिषे (*dadhiṣe*)—you make for yourself

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Lord Soma, you who are pressed by the pressing stones flow, crossing over the ten-fold strainer with its woollen fibres. As a townsman enters a town, the green-hued Soma enters the vessel standing on the boards. And, O Soma, you provide a seat in those vessels.

### Printed p. 71

##### English translation
Expressed by the stones, Soma (passing) through the sheep's hairs green-tinted, entering the cups as a man (enters) a city, you take your seat in the wooden (vessels).

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स मा॑मृजे ति॒रो अण्वा॑नि मे॒ष्यो॑ मि॒ळ्ळे सप्ति॒र्न वा॑ज॒युः ।  
> अ॒नु॒माद्यः॒ पव॑मानो मनी॒षिभिः॒ सोमो॒ विप्रे॑भि॒र्युक्व॑भिः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । ममृजे । तिरः । अण्वानि । मेष्यः । मिळ्हे । सप्तिः । न । वाजयुः ।  
> अनुमाद्यः । पवमानः । मनीषिभिः ! सोमः । विप्रेभिः । ऋक्वभिः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वाजयुरन्नकामोऽण्वान्यणूनि सूक्ष्माणि मेष्यो मेष्या अवे रोमाणि
> पवित्राणि तिरस्कुर्वन् स सोमो ममृजे । परिशोध्यते । अलंक्रियते वा । तत्र
> दृष्टांतः । सप्तिर्न यथा जयकामैरश्वो मिळ्ळे संग्रामेऽलंक्रियते । कीदृशः ।
> अनुमाद्योऽनुमदनीयः सर्वैः पवमानो मनीषिभिर्यत्विग्भिः पूयमानः
> तथर्क्वभिः॥ छंदसीवनिपाविति वनिप् ॥ स्तुतिमद्भीर्विप्रैर्मेधाविभिरभिष्टुतो मृज्यते॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

वाजयुः (*vājayuḥ*), desirous of food, that Soma, making the अण्वानि (*aṇvāni*), fine मेष्यः (*meṣyaḥ*), sheep's hairs, that is the strainers, pass across, is ममृजे (*mamṛje*), cleansed or adorned. The illustration: as a horse (सप्तिः (*saptiḥ*)) is adorned by those who desire victory in battle (मिळ्हे (*miḷhe*) = संग्रामे (*saṃgrāme*)). Of what kind is he? अनुमाद्यः (*anumādyaḥ*), to be rejoiced in by all; पवमानः (*pavamānaḥ*), purified by the मनीषिभिः (*manīṣibhiḥ*), the priests; likewise by the ऋक्वभिः (*ṛkvabhiḥ*), those possessing praise, the wise विप्रः (*vipraḥ*), the intelligent, he is praised and is cleansed.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- माळ्ही (*māḷhī*)—in battle **NOTE**
- सप्तिः न (*saptiḥ na*)—as a horse is adorned
- वाजयुः (*vājayuḥ*)—desirous of food
- सः सोमः (*saḥ somaḥ*)—that Lord Soma
- अण्वानि (*aṇvāni*)—fine
- मेष्यः (*meṣyaḥ*)—woollen threads
- तिरः (*tiraḥ*)—having crossed
- ममृजे (*mamṛje*)—is adorned
- अनुमाद्यः (*anumādyaḥ*)—one praised by all
- मनीषिभिः (*manīṣibhiḥ*)—by the intelligent
- ऋक्वभिः (*ṛkvabhiḥ*)—by the praisers
- पवमानः (*pavamānaḥ*)—Soma, who is purified, is adorned

### Printed p. 72

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As a horse is adorned in battle, Lord Soma, who is praised by all, purified by intelligent praisers and desirous of food, is adorned, having crossed the woollen threads.

##### English translation
Desirous of (bestowing) food, passing through the interstices of the fleece the Soma is adorned like a horse in the battle; the Pavamana, to be rejoiced in (by all, praised) by the intelligent, the sages, the reciters of sacred songs.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र सो॑म दे॒ववी॑तये॒ सिंधु॒र्न पि॑प्ये॒ अर्ण॑सा ।  
> अं॒शोः पय॑सा मदि॒रो न जागृ॑वि॒रच्चा॒ कोशं॑ मधु॒श्चुतं॑॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । सोम । देववीतये । सिंधुः । न । पिप्ये । अर्णसा ।  
> अंशोः । पयसा । मदिरः । स । जागृविः । अच्छ । कोशं । मधुश्चुतं ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम देवनीतये देवानां पानाय तदर्थमर्णसा वसतीवर्याख्येनोदकेन प्र पिप्ये । प्रप्यायसे । तत्र दृष्टांतः । सिंधुर्न यथा सिंधुरुदकेन
> प्रपिप्ये तद्वत् ॥ प्यायतेर्लिटि लिड्यजोश्चति पीभावः ॥ ततः स त्वं मदिरो न मदकरः सुरादिरिव जागृविर्जागरणशीलः । यद्वा । नः संप्रत्यर्थे । इदानीं मदकरो
> जागरणशीलस्त्वं अंशोर्लताखंडस्य पयसा रसेन मधुश्चुतं रसस्य क्षारयितारं
> कोशं द्रोणकलशमच्छाभिगच्छसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, for the देववीतये (*devavītaye*) (printed देवनीतये (*devanītaye*) in the bhāṣya), the drinking of the gods, you swell forth (प्र पिप्ये (*pra pipye*)) with the water called *vasatīvarī* (अर्णसा (*arṇasā*)). The illustration: as a river (सिंधुः (*siṃdhuḥ*)) swells with water. Then you, मदिरः (*madiraḥ*), intoxicating like liquor, are जागृविः (*jāgṛviḥ*), given to wakefulness. Or else, न (*na*) is in the sense of "now": now you, the intoxicating, wakeful one, go (अच्छ (*accha*)) with the पयसा (*payasā*), the juice of the अंशोः (*aṃśoḥ*), the plant-stalk, to the कोशं (*kośaṃ*), the Droṇa vessel, which makes the sap flow, मधुश्चुतं (*madhuścutaṃ*).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O Lord Soma
- सिंधुः ग (*siṃdhuḥ ga*)—as a river swells with waters **NOTE**
- (त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you)
- देववीतये (*devavītaye*)—for the drinking of the gods
- अर्णसा (*arṇasā*)—with the waters called *vasatīvarī* …  **NOTE**

### Printed p. 73
#### Ṛk 12 (continued)

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- गूडि (*gūḍi*)—together with
- प्र पिप्ये (*pra pipye*)—you grow
- मदिरो न (*madiro na*)—like intoxicating liquors and the like
- जागृविः (*jāgṛviḥ*)—you, the vigilant one
- अंशोः (*aṃśoḥ*)—of the plant
- पयसा (*payasā*)—with the juice
- मधुश्चुतं (*madhuścutaṃ*)—that drips sweet juice
- कोशं अच्छ (*kośaṃ accha*)—you go to the Droṇa-vessel and join it

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, as a river grows with its waters, you grow for the gods' drinking, joined with the waters of the vasatīvarī and the like. Vigilant like intoxicating liquors and the like, you go, with the juice of your plant, to the Droṇa-vessel that drips sweet juice, and join it.

##### English translation
You are fed with water, Soma, like a river, for the banquet of the gods ; with the juice of your fi'ament you go to honey-dropping receptacle, exhilarating, vigilant-

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ ह॑र्य॒तो अर्जु॑ने॒ अत्के॑ अव्यत प्रि॒यः सू॒नुर्न मर्ज्यः॑ ।  
> तमीं॑ हिन्वंत्य॒पसो॒ यथा॒ रथं॑ न॒दीष्टा गभ॑स्तोः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । हर्यतः । अर्जुने । अत्के । अव्यत । प्रियः । सूनुः । न । मर्ज्यः ।  
> तं । ईं । हिन्वंति । अपसः । यथा । रथं । नदीषु ! आ । गभस्तोः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हर्यतः स्पृहणीयः प्रियः प्रीणयिता सूनुर्न सूनुरिव मर्जो
> मार्जनीयः सोमोर्जुनेजुनवर्णॆत्क रूपे पवित्र आवृत । अवृणोति ।
> तमीमेनं सोममंगुलयो नदीषु नदमानासु वसतीवरीषु गभस्त्योर्बाहोराभिमुख्येन हिन्वंति । प्रेरयंति । तत्र दृष्टांतः । अपसो यथा वेगवंतो
> जना रथं संग्रामेषु प्रेरयंति तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Harya­taḥ means desirable; priyaḥ, one who pleases. Like a son, Soma, who is to be cleansed (मर्ज्यः (*marjyaḥ*)), is covered in the pavitra, in the bright (अर्जुने (*arjune*)) form (अत्के (*atke*)). The *aṃgulayaḥ* (fingers) urge this Soma forward with the arms, towards the front, into the streams, that is, the roaring vasatīvarī waters. The illustration: as swift men urge a chariot forward in battles, so they do.

### Printed p. 74

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हरतः (*harataḥ*)—attractive
- प्रियः (*priyaḥ*)—pleasing
- सूनुः न (*sūnuḥ na*)—like a son
- मर्ज्यः (*marjyaḥ*)—Soma, who is fit to be purified
- अर्जुने (*arjune*)—of bright colour
- अत्के (*atke*)—with cloth (the wool of the pavitra)
- आ अव्यत (*ā avyata*)—is covered and adorned
- अपसः (*apasaḥ*)—the powerful
- रथं यथा (*rathaṃ yathā*)—as they urge their chariots to battle
- तमीं (*tamīṃ*)—this very Soma
- नदीषु (*nadīṣu*)—into the vasatīvarī waters and the like
- गभस्तोः (*gabhastoḥ*)—with their arms
- आ हिन्वंति (*ā hinvaṃti*)—they urge and send

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Like an attractive and pleasing son, Soma, fit to be purified, is covered and adorned with a bright cloth (the wool of the ten-fold pavitra). As the powerful urge their chariots to battle, they urge this same Soma with their arms into the vasatīvarī waters and the like.

##### English translation
Agreeable affectionate like a son about to be decorated, the Soma is clad in a shining robe ; they send him with their arms into the streams as active (warriors send their) chariot into (battle).

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भि सोमा॑स आ॒यवः॒ पवं॑ते॒ मद्यं॒ मदं॑ ।  
> स॒मु॒द्रस्याधि॑ वि॒ष्टपि॑ मनी॒षिणो॑ मत्स॒रासः॑ स्व॒र्विदः॑ ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । सोमासः । आयवः । पवंते । मद्यं । मॆदं ।  
> समुद्रस्य । अधि । विष्टपि । मनीषिणः । मत्सरासः ' स्वःविद? ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आयवो गमनशीला सोमासः सोमा मद्यं मदकरं मदमात्मीयं
> रसमभि पवंते । अभितो निर्गमयंति । कुत्रेति उच्यते । समुद्रस्यांतरिक्षस्याधि
> विष्टप्यधिकं समुच्छ्रिते पवित्रे । यद्वा । समुद्रस्य । समुद्रवंत्यस्माद्रसाः । तस्य

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Somas, आयवः (*āyavaḥ*) (swift-going), send forth their own intoxicating juice on every side. Where? Upon the विष्टपि (*viṣṭapi*), the raised pavitra, of the समुद्रस्य (*samudrasya*), the firmament. Or else: समुद्रस्य (*samudrasya*) means of that from which the juices flow together, namely the vessel; they send them forth upon its upper place, the pavitra.

### Printed p. 75

> कलशस्याध्युपरि विष्टपि स्थाने पवित्रे निर्गमयंति । कीदृशाः । मनीषिणो मनस
> ई शितारो मत्सरासो मदकराः स्वर्विदः सर्वस्य लंभकाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Of what kind are they? मनीषिणः (*manīṣiṇaḥ*), rulers of the mind; मत्सरासः (*matsarāsaḥ*), intoxicating; स्वर्विदः (*svarvidaḥ*), obtainers of everything.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मनीषिणि (*manīṣiṇi*)—having excellent resolve
- मत्सरासः (*matsarāsaḥ*)—gladdening
- स्वर्विदः (*svarvidaḥ*)—bringing everything within reach
- आयवः (*āyavaḥ*)—swift-going
- सोमाः (*somāḥ*)—the Somas
- मद्यं (*madyaṃ*)—beneficial
- मदं (*madaṃ*)—their juice
- समुद्रस्य अधि (*samudrasya adhi*)—above the firmament
- विष्टपि (*viṣṭapi*)—in the elevated region
- अभि पवंते (*abhi pavaṃte*)—they cause to flow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The swift Somas, having excellent resolve, gladdening, bringing everything within reach, cause their beneficial juice to flow in the elevated region above the firmament.

##### English translation
The swift Somas send forth their inebriating juice upon the uplifted (filter) of the firmament, intelligent, exhilarating, all-acquiring.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तर॑त्समु॒द्रं पव॑मान ऊ॒र्मिणा॒ राजा॑ दे॒व ऋ॒तं बृ॒हत् ।  
> अर्ष॑न्मि॒त्रस्य॒ वरु॑णस्य॒ धर्म॑णा॒ प्र हि॑न्वा॒न ऋ॒तं बृ॒हत् ॥१५॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तरत् । समुद्रं । पवमानः । ऊर्मिणा । राजा । देवः । ऋतं । बृहत् ।  
> अर्षत् । मित्रस्य । वरुणस्य । धर्मणा । प्र । हिन्वानः । ऋतं । बृहत् ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पवमानः । पूयमानो देवो द्योतमानो बृहदत्यंतमृतं सत्यभूतो
> राजा सोमः समुद्रमंतरिक्षं कलशं वोर्मिणा धारया तरत् । तरति । हिन्वानः
> प्रेर्यमाण ऋतं बृहदत्यंतं सत्यभूतः स सोमो मित्रस्य वरुणस्य मित्रावरुणयोर्धर्मणा धारणार्थं प्रार्षत् । प्रगच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, being purified, shining, exceedingly great, true by nature, a king, crosses the समुद्रं (*samudraṃ*), the firmament or the vessel, with his stream (ऊर्मिणा (*ūrmiṇā*)). Being urged forward, great and true, that Soma goes forth for the support of Mitra and Varuṇa, that is, of the pair Mitra-Varuṇa.

### Printed p. 76

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पवमानः (*pavamānaḥ*)—he who is purified
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—shining
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—exceedingly great
- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—true by nature
- राजा (*rājā*)—Soma, the lord
- समुद्रं (*samudraṃ*)—the firmament
- ऊर्मिणा (*ūrmiṇā*)—with his stream
- तरत् (*tarat*)—crosses
- हिन्वानः (*hinvānaḥ*)—urged by the priests
- बृहत् ऋतं (*bṛhat ṛtaṃ*)—Soma, exceedingly of the nature of truth
- मित्रस्य (*mitrasya*)—for Mitra
- वरुणस्य (*varuṇasya*)—for Varuṇa
- धर्मणा (*dharmaṇā*)—for support
- प्रार्षत् (*prārṣat*)—flows forth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, the purified, shining, exceedingly great, true by nature, the lord, crosses the firmament with his stream. Urged by the priests, exceedingly of the nature of truth, he flows forth for the support of Mitra and Varuṇa.

##### English translation
The divine royal (Soma) vast and true, undergoing purification traverses the firmament in a stream ; vast and true, being sent forth he flows for the support of Mitra and Varuna.

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नृभि॑र्येमा॒नो ह॑र्य॒तो वि॑चक्ष॒णो राजा॑ दे॒वः स॑मु॒द्रियः॑ ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नृभिः । येमानः । हर्यतः । विचक्षणः । राजा । देवः । समुद्रियः ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नृभिः कर्मनेतृभिर्येमानो नियम्यमानो हर्यतः स्पृहणीयो
> विचक्षणो विद्रष्टा देवो दीप्यमानः समुद्रियोऽंतरिक्षे भवो राजा सोम
> इंद्रार्थं पवत इत्युत्तरेण संबंधः ॥ समुद्राभ्राद्धः इति भवार्थे घप्रत्ययः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Regulated by the नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*), the leaders of the rite; हर्यतः (*haryataḥ*), desirable; विचक्षणः (*vicakṣaṇaḥ*), the far-seeing; देवः (*devaḥ*), shining; समुद्रियः (*samudriyaḥ*), born in the firmament; the king Soma flows for Indra. The connection is with the following verse. The affix घ (*gha*) is in the sense of "born in" (भवार्थे (*bhavārthe*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—by the priests
- येमानः (*yemānaḥ*)—regulated
