The auspicious (Soma) sustains Indra's body by which he has slain all Rakshasas.

### Printed p. 116
#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पिबं॑त्यस्य॒ विश्वे॑ दे॒वासो॒ गोभिः॑ स्री॒तस्य॒ नृभिः॑ सु॒तस्य॑ ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पिबंति । अस्य । विश्वे । देवासः । गोभिः । श्रीतस्य । नृभिः । सुतस्य ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विश्वे सर्वॆ देवासो देवा गोभिर्गॊविकारैः श्रीतस्य मिश्रितं नृभिः कर्मनेतृभिर्युत्विग्भिः सुतास्याभिषुतमस्यामुं सोमं पिबंति । द्वितीयार्थे षष्ठ्यः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

All the gods drink this Soma, mixed (श्रीतस्य (*śrītasya*)) with cow-products (गोविकारैः (*govikāraiḥ*)) and pressed by the priests, the leaders of the rite. The genitives are used in the sense of the accusative.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्वे (*viśve*)—all
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—the gods
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—with milk and the like
- श्रीतस्य (*śrītasya*)—mixed
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—by the priests
- सुतस्य (*sutasya*)—pressed
- अस्य (*asya*)—this Soma
- पिबंति (*pibaṃti*)—drink

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

All the gods drink this Soma, mixed with milk and the like and pressed by the priests.

##### English translation
The universal gods drink of it, effused by the priests, mixed with the milk and curds.

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र सु॑वा॒नो अ॑क्षाः स॒हस्र॑धारास्ति॒रः प॒वित्रं॒ वि वार॒मव्यं॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । सुवासः । अक्षारिति । सहस्रधारः । तिरः । पवित्रं । वि । वारं । अव्यं ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुवानोऽभिषूयमाणः अत एव सहस्रधारो बहुधारायुक्तः सोमोऽव्यमविभवं वारं वालं पवित्रं तिरो व्यवधायकं कुर्वन् प्राक्षाः । विविधं प्रक्षरति ॥
> क्षरतेर्लुजि रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, being pressed and therefore possessed of a thousand streams (many streams), makes the sheep's wool (*avyaṃ vāraṃ*) filter a screen (तिरः (*tiraḥ*)) and flows (प्राक्षाः (*prākṣāḥ*)) in manifold ways. अक्षाः (*akṣāḥ*) is the form of the root क्षर (*kṣara*) in the aorist with luk.

### Printed p. 117

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुवानः (*suvānaḥ*)—pressed
- सहस्रधारः (*sahasradhāraḥ*)—having many kinds of streams; Soma
- अव्यं (*avyaṃ*)—of wool
- वारं (*vāraṃ*)—having threads
- पवित्रं (*pavitraṃ*)—the ten-fringed filter
- तिरः (*tiraḥ*)—crossing
- वि प्र अक्षाः (*vi pra akṣāḥ*)—flows in various forms

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, pressed and with many kinds of streams, crosses the ten-fringed filter of woollen thread and flows in various forms (in many streams).

##### English translation
Being effused the The Soma in a thousand streams flows through the sheep's-hair filter.

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स वा॒ज्य॑क्षाः स॒हस्र॑रेता अ॒द्भिर्मृ॑जा॒नो गोभिः॑ श्रीणा॒नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । वाजी । अक्षारिति । सहस्ररेताः आर्‍भीः । मृजानः । गोभिः । श्रीणानः ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वाजी वेगवान्बलवान्वा स सोमोऽक्षाः । क्षरति । कीदृशः । सहस्ररेता बहुरेतस्नो बहूदकोऽद्भिर्वसतीवरीभिर्मृजानो मृज्यमानो गोभिगोर्गोविकारैः
> क्षेरादिभिः श्रीणानः श्रीयमाणः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The vigorous (swift or strong) Soma flows. Of what kind? सहस्ररेता (*sahasraretā*): of abundant juice, with much water; cleansed (मृजानः (*mṛjānaḥ*)) with the waters, the वसतीवरी (*vasatīvarī*) waters; and mixed (श्रीणानः (*śrīṇānaḥ*)) with cow-products, milk and the like.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सहस्ररेताः (*sahasraretāḥ*)—of abundant juice
- अद्भिः (*adbhiḥ*)—with the Vasatīvarī waters and the like
- मृजानः (*mṛjānaḥ*)—undergoing purification
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—with milk and the like
- श्रीणानः (*śrīṇānaḥ*)—undergoing mixture
- वाजी (*vājī*)—swift
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Soma
- अक्षाः (*akṣāḥ*)—flows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

That same god Soma, of abundant juice, purified with the Vasatīvarī waters and the like, mixed with milk and the like, and swift, flows.

##### English translation
The vigorous Soma flows in a thousand streams cleansed by the waters and mixing with the curds and milk,

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र सो॑म या॒हींद्र॑स्य कु॒क्षा नृभि॑र्येमा॒नो अद्रि॑भिः  ॒
> सुतः ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । सोम । याहि । इंद्रस्य । कुक्षा । नृभिः । येमानः । अद्रिभिः । सुतः ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम नृभिर्ऋग्विघ्र्येमानो नियम्यमानोऽद्रिभिर्गावभिः सुतोऽभिसुतस्त्वमिंद्रस्य कुक्षा ॥ सप्तम्या डादेशः ॥ कुक्षावुदरभूते कलशे वा प्र याहि ।
> प्रकर्षण गच्छ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, restrained (येमानः (*yemānaḥ*)) by the priests and pressed with the stones (अद्रिभिः (*adribhiḥ*), the pressing stones), go forth into Indra's belly (कुक्षा (*kukṣā*), with डा (*ḍā*) substituted for the locative) or into the vessel that is like a belly; go forward.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O god Soma
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—by the priests
- येमानः (*yemānaḥ*)—restrained
- अद्रिभिः (*adribhiḥ*)—by the pressing stones
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—pressed; you
- इंद्रस्य (*iṃdrasya*)—of Indra
- कुक्षा (*kukṣā*)—into the belly
- प्र याहि (*pra yāhi*)—enter and go

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O god Soma, restrained by the priests and crushed and pressed with the pressing stones, enter and go into Indra's belly.

##### English translation
Guided by the priests and eflused with the stones go, Soma, to Indra's belly.

#### Ṛk 19

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अस॑र्जि वा॒जी ति॒रः प॒वित्र॒मिंद्रा॑य॒ सोमः॑ स॒हस्र॑धारः ॥१९॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आसर्जि । वाजी तिरः । पवित्रं । इंद्राय ! सोमः । सहस्रधातु ॥ १९ ॥

### Printed p. 119

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वाजी बलवान्वेगवान्ना पवित्रं तिरस्कुर्वन् सहस्रधारो बहुधारः सोम
> इंद्रायेंद्रार्थमसर्जि । सृज्यते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, vigorous (strong or swift), passing the filter, with a thousand (many) streams, has been sent forth (असर्जि (*asarji*), is released) for Indra.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वाजी (*vājī*)—endowed with strength
- पवित्रं तिरः (*pavitraṃ tiraḥ*)—crossing the ten-fringed filter
- सहस्रधारः (*sahasradhāraḥ*)—flowing in many streams
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—the god Soma
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for Indra (is released)
- अस्पृजि (*aspṛji*)—flows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The god Soma, endowed with strength, crossing the ten-fringed filter and flowing in many streams, flows for Indra's drinking.

##### English translation
The vigorous thousand-streamed Soma has been sent through the filter for Indra.

#### Ṛk 20

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अं॒जंत्ये॑नं॒ मध्वो॒ रसे॒नेंद्रा॑य॒ वृष्ण॒ इंदुं॒ मदा॑य ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अंजंति, एनं मध्व । रसेन । इंद्राय वृष्णे । इंदुं मदाय ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वृष्टे कामानां वर्षित्र इंद्रायेंद्रस्य मदाय मदार्थं मध्यो
> मधुनो रसेन गव्येनैनमिंदं सोममृत्विजो०जंति । संयोजयंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

For the exhilaration of Indra, the showerer (वृष्णे (*vṛṣṇe*)) of desires, the priests anoint (अंजंति (*aṃjaṃti*)), that is, join together, this Soma with the juice (रसेन (*rasena*)) of the sweet (मध्वः (*madhvaḥ*)), the juice made from the cow.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृष्णे (*vṛṣṇe*)—the granter of desired things
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—of Indra
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for exhilaration
- मध्वः (*madhvaḥ*)—sweet
- रसेन (*rasena*)—with the juice
- एनं इंदुं (*enaṃ iṃduṃ*)—this Soma
- अंजंति (*aṃjaṃti*)—the priests mix

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

For the exhilaration of Indra, the granter of desired things, the priests mix this Soma with sweet juice.

### Printed p. 120

##### English translation
They mix the Soma with the liquid of the sweet (milk) for Indra, the showerer (of benefits) for his exhilaration.

#### Ṛk 21

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दे॒वेभ्य॑स्ता॒ वृथा॒ पाज॑से॒ऽपो वसा॑नं॒ हरिं॑ मृजंति ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> देवेभ्यः । त्वा । वृथा । पाजसे । अपः । वसानं । हरिं । मृजंति ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम अप उदकानि वसानमाच्छादयंतं हरिं हरितवर्णं त्वा त्वां
> वृथानायासेनर्विजो मृजंति । शोधयंति । किमर्थं । देवेभ्यो देवानां
> पानार्थं पाजसे तेषां बलार्थं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, you who clothe (वसानं (*vasānaṃ*), cover) yourself with the waters, green (हरिं (*hariṃ*)) in colour: the priests cleanse (मृजंति (*mṛjaṃti*)) you readily (वृथा (*vṛthā*), without effort). For what purpose? For the gods, for their drinking (पाजसे (*pājase*)), for their strength.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अपः (*apaḥ*)—the waters
- वसानं (*vasānaṃ*)—one who is clothed in
- हरिं (*hariṃ*)—one of green colour
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- वृथा (*vṛthā*)—without effort
- देवेभ्यः (*devebhyaḥ*)—of the gods
- पाजसे (*pājase*)—for strength
- मृजंति (*mṛjaṃti*)—the priests cleanse

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O god Soma, you who are clothed in the waters (mixed with them) and are green in colour: the priests cleanse you without effort for the strengthening of the gods.

##### English translation
They cleanse you readily, clothed with water, green-tinted, for the invigoration of the gods.

#### Ṛk 22

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंदु॒रिंद्रा॑य तोषते॒ नि तो॑षते श्री॒णन्नु॒ग्रो रि॒णन्न॒पः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंदुः । इंद्राय । तोशते । नि । तोशते । त्रीणन् । उग्रः । रिणन् । आपः ॥ २२ ॥

### Printed p. 121

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इंदुः सोम इंद्रायेंद्रार्थं तोशते । हस्यते । अभिषूयते । नि
> तोशते । नितरामभिषूयते । तोशतिर्वधकर्मा । कीदृशः । श्रीणन् प्रेरयन्
> सोमोऽभिषूयते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Indu, that is Soma, is effused (तोशते (*tośate*)) for Indra, that is, for Indra's sake; the word is glossed हस्यते (*hasyate*), "is pressed out". नि तोशते (*ni tośate*) means that he is pressed out thoroughly. The root तोशति (*tośati*) has the sense of slaying. What kind of Soma? श्रीणन् (*śrīṇan*) is glossed as "impelling": the Soma is pressed out as one who impels.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- श्रीणन् (*śrīṇan*)—he who is mixed with milk and the like
- उग्र: (*ugra:*)—he who has exceedingly great strength
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—the Vasatīvarī waters and others
- रिणन् (*riṇan*)—he who sets them in motion
- इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*)—Soma
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for Indra's drinking
- तोशते (*tośate*)—is pressed out
- नि तोशते (*ni tośate*)—is pressed out exceedingly

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, who is mixed with milk and the like, who has exceedingly great strength, and who sets the Vasatīvarī waters and others in motion, is pressed out for Indra's drinking; he is pressed out exceedingly.

##### English translation
Indu is effused for Indra, is effused downwards—fierce, mixing (with the milk), flowing (into) the water.

## Sūkta 110 (Maṇḍala 9)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पर्यू ष्टिति द्वादशर्चं सप्तमं सूक्तं । त्रैरुणत्रसदस्य राजर्षि अस्य
> सूक्तस्य द्रष्टारौ । आदितस्तिस्तः पिपीलिकमध्या अनुष्टुभो द्वादशकाष्टकद्वादशकवत्यः ।
> ततः षडूर्ध्वबृहत्यो द्वादशकत्रयवत्यः । अथ दशम्याद्यास्तिस्रो विराजः । पवमानः सोमो देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतं । पर्यू षु दादश त्रैरुणत्रसदस्य
> तिस्रोऽनुष्टुभः पिपीलिकमध्याः षळूर्ध्वबृहत्यस्तिस्त्रत्व विराज इति ॥ गतो विनियोगः #

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning पर्यू ष्टिति (*paryū ṣṭiti*) has twelve ṛks and is the seventh sūkta. Trairuṇa and Trasadasyu, royal sages, are the seers of this sūkta. The first three ṛks are pipīlikamadhyā anuṣṭubh, of twelve, eight and twelve syllables. Then come six ūrdhvabṛhatī verses, each of three twelve-syllable pādas. The three beginning with the tenth are virāj. The deity is Pavamāna Soma. He cites the Anukramaṇī to the same effect, and says that the viniyoga has been given already.

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta, beginning पर्यूषु (*paryūṣu*), is the seventh sūkta in the seventh anuvāka. It has twelve ṛks. Trairuṇa and Trasadasyu, two royal sages, are the seers of the sūkta. The first three ṛks of this sūkta are in the metre called pipīlikamadhyā anuṣṭubh. In this metre there are three pādas: twelve syllables in the first pāda, eight in the second, and twelve in the third. This is the definition of the metre. The six ṛks following the fourth ṛk are in the metre called ūrdhvabṛhatī. In this metre there are three pādas of twelve syllables; this is its definition. The three ṛks following the tenth ṛk are in the virāj metre. Pavamāna Soma is the deity. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated: पर्यूषु द्वादश त्रुणत्रसदस्यो तिस्रो5नुष्टुभः पिपीलिकमध्याः षळूर्थ्वबृहत्यस्ति स्रश्च विराजः (*paryūṣu dvādaśa truṇatrasadasyo tisro5nuṣṭubhaḥ pipīlikamadhyāḥ ṣaḷūrthvabṛhatyasti sraśca virājaḥ*). The viniyoga has been stated earlier.

### Printed p. 122

##### Particulars

Maṇḍala 9; Anuvāka 7; Sūkta 110; Aṣṭaka 7; Adhyāya 5; Varga 23. Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 12. Ṛṣi: Trairuṇa-Trasadasyu. Devatā: Pavamāna Soma. Chandas: 1–3 anuṣṭubh pipīlikamadhyā; 4–9 ūrdhvabṛhatī; 10–12 virāj.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पर्यू॒षु प्र ध॑न्व॒ वाज॑सातये॒ परि॑ वृ॒त्राणि॑ स॒क्षणिः॑।  
> द्वि॒षस्त॒रध्या॑ ऋण॒या न॑ ईयसे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> परि । ऊं इति । सु प्र । धन्व वाजसातये । परि वृत्राणि । सक्षिणिः ।  
> द्विषः । तरध्यै । यणयाः । नः ईयसे । १ .

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ॥ श्रीरामचंद्राय नमः ॥
> हे सोम सु सुस्तु वाजसातयेऽस्मभ्यमन्नदानायैव परि प्र धन्व ! परितः
> प्रगच्छ । यद्वा । वाजसातयेऽन्नलाभाय संग्रामं प्रगच्छ । किं च सक्षणिः सहनशीलस्त्वं वृत्राणि शत्रुन्परि गच्छ । तदेवोच्च्यते नोऽस्माकमृणया ऋणानां
> यापयिता त्वं द्विषः शत्रुंस्तरध्यै शरीतुं हंतुमीयसे । परिगच्छसि :
> १, प्रबंधगळु.
> (ऎलै सोमदेवने) सु—अतिशयवागि । वाजसातॆये—नमगॆ अन्नलाभार्थवागि ।
> हरि प्र धन्व—सुत्तलू प्रवहिसु ! सक्षणि—शत्रुगळन्नु ऎदुरिसुव शक्तियुळ्ळ नीनु. । वृत्राणि—

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Soma, सु (*su*), exceedingly, flow all round (परि प्र धन्व (*pari pra dhanva*)) for वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*), that is, for giving us food; go forth on every side." Or else: "for वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*), the gain of food, go to battle." Further: "you, सक्षणिः (*sakṣaṇiḥ*), of enduring nature, go round the वृत्राणि (*vṛtrāṇi*), the enemies." The same is said again: "you, ऋणया (*ṛṇayā*), the remover of our debts, go (ईयसे (*īyase*)) to destroy (तरध्यै (*taradhyai*)) the enemies (द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)); you go round them."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सु (*su*)—(O Soma) exceedingly
- वाजसातॆये (*vājasātèye*)—for our gain of food
- हरि प्र धन्व (*hari pra dhanva*)—flow all round
- सक्षणि (*sakṣaṇi*)—you who have the power to face enemies
- वृत्राणि (*vṛtrāṇi*)—the enemies

### Printed p. 123
- परि (*pari*)—go, encompassing them
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- ऋणयाः (*ṛṇayāḥ*)—you who repay our debts
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—our enemies
- तरध्यै (*taradhyai*)—destroy
- ईयसे (*īyase*)—go forward

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, flow all round for our gain of food. You who have the power to face enemies, go encompassing the enemies. You who repay our debts, go forward to destroy our enemies.

##### English translation
Hasten, Soma, to bring us sustenance; gifted with endurance (attack our) foes ; the acquitter of our debts, you approach to disperse our enemies.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अनु॒ हि त्वा॑ सु॒तं सो॑म॒ मदा॑मसि म॒हे स॑मर्य॒राज्ये॑ ।  
> वाजा॑ अ॒भि प॑वमान॒ प्र गा॑हसे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अनु । हि । त्वा । सुतं । सोम । मदामसि । महे । समर्यराज्ये :  
> वाजन् । अभि ; पवमान । प्र । गाहसे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम सुतमभिषुतं त्वा त्वां वयमनु मदामसि हि । अनुमदामः ।
> अनुक्रमेणाभिष्टुमः खलु । हे पवमान सोम स त्वं महे महति समर्यराज्ये महत्समनुष्यं त्वदीयं राज्यमनुपालयितं वाजाइत्युबलान्यभिलक्ष्य प्र
> गाहसे । प्रगच्छसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Soma, we praise you (मदामसि (*madāmasi*)) who have been pressed (सुतं (*sutaṃ*)), in due order; we eulogise you indeed." "O Pavamāna Soma, you, to protect your own great sovereignty over men (महे समर्यराज्ये (*mahe samaryarājye*)), advance (प्र गाहसे (*pra gāhase*)), aiming at the वाजान् (*vājān*), the forces."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O Soma
- सुतं (*sutaṃ*)—who have been pressed
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- अनुमदामसि हि. (*anumadāmasi hi.*)—we praise
- पवमान (*pavamāna*)—O Soma, called Pavamāna
- महे (*mahe*)—great

### Printed p. 124
- समर‍्ये राज्ये (*samarye rājye*)—to protect your sovereignty among men
- वाजान् अभि (*vājān abhi*)—facing the forces of the enemy
- प्र गाहसे (*pra gāhase*)—you charge forward

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, we praise you who have been pressed. O Soma, called Pavamāna, you charge forward against the forces of the enemy in order to protect your great sovereignty among men.

##### English translation
In turn we glorify you, Soma, when effused; you attack the (hostile) forces, O Pavamana, to (defend) your great sovereignty over men.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अजी॑जनो॒ हि प॑वमान॒ सूर्यं॑ वि॒धारे॒ शक्म॑ना॒  ॑
> पयः ।  
> गोजी॑रया॒ रंह॑माण॒ पुरं॑ध्या ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अजीजनः । हि । पवमान । सूर्यं । विधारे । शक्मना । पयः ।  
> गोर्जीरया । रंहमाणः । पुरंध्या । ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे पवमान सोम त्वं पयः पयस उदकस्य विधारे विधारकेंतरिक्षे
> शक्कना समर्थेन बलेन सूर्यमजीजनो हि । उत्पादितवान्भवसि खलु । कीदृशः !
> गोजीरया स्तोतृभ्यो गवां प्रेरकेण । स्तोतृणां प्रेरितपशुकेनेत्यर्थः ।
> तादृशेन पुरंध्या बहुविधप्रज्ञानेन युक्तो रंहमाणो वेगं कुर्वाणस्त्वं
> सूर्यमजीजनः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Pavamāna Soma, you have generated the sun (सूर्यमजीजनो हि (*sūryamajījano hi*)) in the sky (विधारे (*vidhāre*)), the sustainer of पयः (*payaḥ*), water, by your capable strength (शक्कना (*śakkanā*))." What sort of one? गोजीरया (*gojīrayā*) is explained as "by the impeller of cattle to the worshippers", that is, one by whom cattle are sent to the worshippers. Endowed with such पुरंध्या (*puraṃdhyā*), manifold wisdom, and speeding (रंहमाणः (*raṃhamāṇaḥ*)), you generated the sun.
**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पवमान (*pavamāna*)—O Soma, called Pavamāna
- गोजीरया (*gojīrayā*)—you who provide cattle for the enemies
- पुरंध्या (*puraṃdhyā*)—you who are endowed with wisdom
- रंहमाणः (*raṃhamāṇaḥ*)—you who are swift

### Printed p. 125
- पयः (*payaḥ*)—for the water
- विधारे (*vidhāre*)—in the sky, the sustainer
- शक्कना (*śakkanā*)—by your capacity
- सूर्यं (*sūryaṃ*)—the sun
- अजीजनो हि (*ajījano hi*)—you have generated, have you not

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, you who provide cattle for the enemies, who are endowed with wisdom and are swift, have generated the sun by your capacity in the sky that sustains the water.

##### English translation
Pavamana, by your might you have generated the sun in (the firmament), the support of the water, hastening on with abundant wisdom that procures cattle (for your worshippers),

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अजी॑जनो अमृत॒ मर्त्ये॒ष्ट्व ऋ॒तस्य॒ धर्म॑न्न॒मृत॑स्य॒  ॑
> चारुणः ।  
> सदा॑सरो॒ वाज॒मच्छा॒ सनि॑ष्यदत् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अजीजनः , अमृत । मर्त्येषु । आ ऋतस्य । धर्मन् । अमृतस्य । चारुणः।  
> सदा । असरः । वाजं । अच्छ । सनिस्यदत् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अमृत : मरधर्मरहित सोम त्वमृतस्य सत्यभूतस्य चारुणः कल्याणस्यामृतस्योदकस्य धर्मन्दारकेंतरिक्षे सूर्यमजीजनः । किमर्थं । मर्त्येष्व
> मनुष्येष्टभिमुखीभवनाय । किंच सनिष्यदत् संभजन् स त्वं वाजमच्छ संग्राममभिलक्ष्य सदासरः । सरसि । गच्छसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O immortal (अमृत (*amṛta*)), Soma, free from the nature of death, you generated the sun in the sky (धर्मन् (*dharman*)), the sustainer of ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*), the true, चारुणः (*cāruṇaḥ*), the auspicious, अमृतस्य (*amṛtasya*), ambrosial water." For what purpose? "मर्त्येषु (*martyeṣu*), in order to face mortals, men." Further: "you, सनिष्यदत् (*saniṣyadat*), bestowing, always (सदा (*sadā*)) go (असरः (*asaraḥ*)), aiming at वाजं (*vājaṃ*), battle."
**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अमृत (*amṛta*)—O Soma, free from the nature of death
- यतस्य (*yatasya*)—of the water that is of the nature of truth
- चारुणः (*cāruṇaḥ*)—and of the nature of auspiciousness
- अमृतस्य (*amṛtasya*)—and everlasting
- धर्मन् (*dharman*)—in the sky, the sustainer

### Printed p. 126
- मर्त्येषु आ (*martyeṣu ā*)—to appear before men
- अजीजनः (*ajījanaḥ*)—you generated the sun
- सनिष्यदत् (*saniṣyadat*)—you who flow majestically
- वाजं अच्छ (*vājaṃ accha*)—facing the battle
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- असरः (*asaraḥ*)—you charge forward

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, free from the nature of death, you generated the sun, in the sky that sustains the water that is true, auspicious and everlasting, so as to appear before men. You who flow majestically always charge forward towards the battle.

##### English translation
Immortal Soma, you have generated (the sun) among mortals in (the firmament), the support of the truthful auspicious ambrosia ; streaming forth you go to battle continually.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भ्य॑भि॒ हि श्रव॑सा त॒तर्दि॒थोत्सं॒ न कं चि॑ज्जन॒पान॒मक्षि॑तं ।  
> शर्या॑भि॒र्न भर॑माणो॒ गभ॑स्त्योः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभिअभि । हि ' श्रवसा ततर्दिथ । उत्सं । न । कं । चित् । जनपानं ।  
> अक्षितं ।  
> शर्याभिः । न भरमाणः । गभस्त्योः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम त्वं शवसान्नेन हेतुनाभ्यभि त्रतर्दिथ हि ! पवित्रमभितृण्णवानसि ।
> छिन्नवानसि । तत्र दृष्टांतद्वयं । उत्सं न यथा कश्चिज्जनपानं ! अस्मिञ्चना उदकं
> पिबंति । तमक्षितमक्षीणं कं चित् कंचनोत्समुत्सरणशीलं वाप्यादिकमभितृणत्ति

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Soma, by शवसा (*śavasā*), with food as the cause, you have pierced right through (*abhyabhi tratarditha*): you have cut through the filter." Here there are two illustrations. First, "like a spring (उत्सं न (*utsaṃ na*))", as some जनपानं (*janapānaṃ*), a place where people drink water: that unexhausted, imperishable (अक्षितं (*akṣitaṃ*)) one, any (कं चित् (*kaṃ cit*)) spring, a well or the like of welling nature, he pierces.
**NOTE**

### Printed p. 127

> अथवा कश्चिद्गभस्तोर्बाह्वोः शर्याभिरंगुलीभिर्भरमाण उदकं संभरन् कंचिदभितृणत्ति तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa gives a second explanation: or else someone, carrying water with the fingers (शर्याः (*śaryāḥ*)) of his two arms (गभस्त्योः (*gabhastyoḥ*)), pours it forth and breaks open a spring-like source; the Soma is like him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जनपानं (*janapānaṃ*)—fit to be drunk by the people of the world
- अक्षितं (*akṣitaṃ*)—inexhaustible
- कं चित् उत्सं न (*kaṃ cit utsaṃ na*)—as one bursts open a spring of water
- भरमाण् (*bharamāṇ*)—one who is carrying water
- गभस्त्यो (*gabhastyo*)—of his arms
- शराभिः (*śarābhiḥ*)—with the fingers, as one sprinkles water
- श्रवसा (*śravasā*)—for the sake of food
- अभ्यभि (*abhyabhi*)—all around the filter
- ततर्दिथ हि (*tatarditha hi*)—you have split it

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, you have split the filter all around, for the sake of food, as one breaks open a spring of water that people of the world can drink and that cannot dry up, to make the water rise up; and as one who carries water sprinkles it with the fingers of his arms.

##### English translation
You have burst open (the filter) with (your) food as (one bursts open) an inexhaustible fountain giving drink to the nation, like one taking up (water) with the fingers of his two hands.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Śaryāḥ—explaining the meaning of this word, Yāska says in his Nirukta: शर्याः । शर्या अंगुलयो भवंति । सृजंति कर्माणि । शर्या इषवः शरमय्यः । शरः शृणातेः । शर्याभिर्न भरमाणो गभस्तो इत्यपि निगमो भवति ॥ (*śaryāḥ | śaryā aṃgulayo bhavaṃti | sṛjaṃti karmāṇi | śaryā iṣavaḥ śaramayyaḥ | śaraḥ śṛṇāteḥ | śaryābhirna bharamāṇo gabhasto ityapi nigamo bhavati ‖*) (Ni. 5-4). Śaryāḥ means aṅguli, fingers. Because work is done with the help of these fingers, the word śaryāḥ indicates fingers. And this word has another meaning too, arrows. The word śara is derived from the root śṛ (śṛṇāti), "to split", and gives the sense of a weapon that splits and injures. For the word śarya in the sense of "finger", the Vedic text of the present Ṛk itself, शर्याभिर्न भरमाणो गभस्त्योः (*śaryābhirna bharamāṇo gabhastyoḥ*), is the example.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आदीं॒ के चि॒त्पश्य॑मानास॒ आप्यं॑ वसु॒रुचो॑ दि॒व्या  ॒॑
> अ॒भ्य॑नूषत ।  
> वारं॒ न दे॒वः स॑वि॒ता व्यू॑र्णुते ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आत् । ईं । के । चित् । पश्यमानासः । अप्यं । वसुरुचः । दिव्याः । अभि ।  
> अनूषत ।  
> वारं न । देवः । सविता । वि । ऊर्णुते ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आदनंतरं पश्यमानास एनं पश्यंतो दिव्या दिवि भवा वसुरुचो नाम के
> चिदाप्यं बंधुं साधुमीमेनं सोममभ्यनूषत । अभ्यस्तुवन् । कस्मादनंतरमिति
> उच्यते । देवो द्योतमानः सविता सर्वस्य प्रेरकः सूर्यो नारमावरकमंधकारं न व्यूर्णुते । नापगमयति । तदैनमस्तुवन् । सूर्योदयात्पागेव हि
> सोमं स्तुवंति खलु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Thereafter, seeing him, certain celestial beings (दिव्या (*divyā*)), born in heaven and named Vasuruc, praised this Soma as a kinsman (आप्यं (*āpyaṃ*)), a good friend. Why "thereafter"? The shining Savitṛ, the sun, the impeller of all, does not yet remove the obstructing (वारं (*vāraṃ*)) darkness. So they praised him then; for they praise Soma before sunrise.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आत् (*āt*)—immediately after that
- देवः (*devaḥ*)—the shining
- सविता (*savitā*)—the sun
- वारं (*vāraṃ*)—the covering pitch darkness
- न व्यूर्णुते (*na vyūrṇute*)—(before he) drives away
- पश्यमानासः (*paśyamānāsaḥ*)—those who were looking at this Soma
- दिव्याः (*divyāḥ*)—born in the world of heaven
- वसुरुचः (*vasurucaḥ*)—called Vasuruc
- के चित् (*ke cit*)—some
- आप्यं (*āpyaṃ*)—in the form of a kinsman
- ईं (*īṃ*)—this Soma
- अभ्यनूषत (*abhyanūṣata*)—praised

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As soon as Soma appeared, some people who were looking at this Soma, born in heaven and called Vasuruc, praised this Soma as a kinsman, before the shining sun rose and drove away the pitch darkness.

##### English translation
Beholding him certain celestial Varuruchas praise him as a kinsman before the shining Savitri drives away the obstructing (darkness).

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वे सो॑म प्रथ॒मा वृ॒क्तब॑र्हिषो म॒हे वाजा॑य॒ श्रव॑से॒ ॑ 
> धियं दधुः ।  
> x त्वं नो॑ वीर वी॒र्या॑य चोदय ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वे इति । सोम । प्रथमाः । वृक्तबर्हिषः । महे । वाजाय । श्रवसे । धियं ।  
> दधुः ।  
> सः । त्वं । नः । वीर । पीयार्याय । जोदय । ७  
> ३ १. २. ३.  
> हे सोम प्रथमाः पुरातनः यद्वा ! यष्टृत्वेन सर्वेषां जनानां  
> मुख्याः । व्यक्तबर्हिषः । व्यक्तं भिन्नं बर्हियैर्यज्ञार्थमिति व्यक्तबर्हिषो  
> यजमानाः । महे महते । वाजाय बलाय श्रवसेन्नाय च धियं बुद्धिं त्वे  
> त्वयि ददुः निहितवंतः । तस्मात् हे वीर समर्थ सोम स तादृशस्त्वं नोऽस्थानपि  
> संग्रामे वीर्याय सामर्थ्याय चोदय । प्रेरय । यद्वा वीर्याय  
> वीरे पुत्रे भवाय सुखायास्मान्स्पेरय  
> प्र.बसवण्ण  
> सोम—ऎल्लॆ सोमदेवने ! प्रथमाः —पुरातनरू वृक्तबर्हिष.—यज्ञक्कागि  
> हरडिद दर्भासनवुळ्ळवरू आद यजमानरु महे—महत्ताद ! वाजाय—बलक्कागियू ।  
> क्रवसे—अन्नक्कागियू धियं—तम्म ध्यानात्मकवाद बुद्धियन्नु । श्वे—निन्नल्लि । दधुः—  
> स्थापिसिद्दारॆ । वीर—सामर्थ्यवुळ्ळ ऎल्लॆ सोमदेवने । सः—प्रसिद्धनाद त्वं—नीनु ।  
> नः—नम्मन्नु ! वीर्याय—शक्ति संपादनॆगागि । चोदय—प्रेरिसु:  
> ॥ धनपद्धति.  
> ऎलै सोमदेवने, पुरातनरू, यज्ञक्कागि धर्भासनवन्नु हरडिरुववरू आद यजमानरु  
> महत्ताद बलक्कागियू, अन्नक्कागियू, तम्म ध्यानात्मकवाद बुद्धियन्नु निन्नल्लि स्थापिसिद्दारॆ.  
> सामर्थ्ययुतनाद ऎलै सोमदेवने, प्रसिद्धनाद नीनु नम्मन्नू सह शक्ति संपादनॆगागि  
> प्रेरिसु  
> xxvii 9

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, the "first" (प्रथमाः (*prathamāḥ*)) are the ancient ones; or else, being sacrificers, the foremost of all people. The व्यक्तबर्हिषः (*vyaktabarhiṣaḥ*) are the sacrificers who have cut the sacred grass for the sacrifice. They have placed their thought (धियं (*dhiyaṃ*)) in you for great strength (वाजाय (*vājāya*)) and food (श्रवसे (*śravase*)). Therefore, O capable hero Soma, such as you are, impel us too in battle toward heroic power. Or else: impel us toward well-being (भवाय (*bhavāya*)), in a heroic son.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O Soma
- प्रथमाः (*prathamāḥ*)—the ancient
- वृक्तबर्हिष. (*vṛktabarhiṣa.*)—sacrificers with the sacred-grass seat spread for the sacrifice
- महे (*mahe*)—great
- वाजाय (*vājāya*)—for strength
- क्रवसे (*kravase*)—for food
- धियं (*dhiyaṃ*)—their meditative thought
- श्वे (*śve*)—in you
- दधुः (*dadhuḥ*)—have placed
- वीर (*vīra*)—O capable Soma
- सः (*saḥ*)—the famous
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- वीर्याय (*vīryāya*)—for the gaining of strength
- चोदय (*codaya*)—impel

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, the sacrificers, the ancient ones who have spread the sacred grass for the sacrifice, have placed their meditative thought in you for great strength and for food. O Soma, endowed with capability, you, the famous one, impel us too toward the gaining of strength.

### Printed p. 130

##### English translation
The chief (of men) having cut the sacred grass have fixed their minds, Soma, on you for abundant strength and food ; do you, O hero, excite us to heroism.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दि॒वः पी॒यूषं॑ पू॒र्व्यं यदु॒क्थ्यं॑ म॒हो गा॒हाद्दि॒न आ  ॑
> निर॑धुक्षत ।  
> इंद्र॑म॒भि जाय॑मानं॒ सम॑स्वरन् ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दिवः । पीयूषं । पूर्व्यं । यत् । उक्त्यं । महः । गाहात् । दिवः । आ । निः ।  
> अधुक्षत ।  
> इंद्रं । अभि । जायमानं । सं । अस्वरन् ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दिवो द्युलोकात् तत्र स्थितैर्देवैः पीयूषं पातव्यं पूर्व्यं प्रत्नं
> यत्सोमरूपमन्नमुक्थं प्रशस्यमस्ति तं सोमं महो महतो गाहाद्गहनाद्दिवो द्युलोकान्निरधुक्षत । अभिमुख्येन निर्दुहंति । ततो दुग्धमिंद्रमभिलक्ष्य
> जायमानं तं सोमं समस्वरन् । स्तोतारः संस्तुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

From heaven, from the great deep heavenly world, they milk out the ancient Soma, which is the food in Soma form, to be drunk by the gods who dwell there and worthy of praise (*ukthaṃ*). They draw it forth facing it. Then, when that Soma is milked out and is being born with Indra as its aim, the singers praise it together.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—for the gods of heaven
- पीयूषं (*pīyūṣaṃ*)—fit to be drunk
- पूर्व्यं (*pūrvyaṃ*)—ancient
- यत् (*yat*)—which Soma
- उक्ष्यं (*ukṣyaṃ*)—is worthy of praise, that Soma
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—great
- गाहात् (*gāhāt*)—deep
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—from the world of heaven
- आ निरधुक्षत (*ā niradhukṣata*)—have called forth
- इंद्रं अभि (*iṃdraṃ abhi*)—for Indra's drinking
- जायमानं (*jāyamānaṃ*)—that Soma, which has been produced
- समस्वरन् (*samasvaran*)—the singers praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

They have called forth, from the great and deep world of heaven, the Soma that is fit for the gods of heaven to drink, ancient and worthy of praise. The singers praise that Soma, produced for Indra's drinking.

### Printed p. 131

##### English translation
They have milked from heaven from the mighty deep the ancient Soma, the beverage (of the gods) of heaven, which is worthy of praise; they have praised it when gencrated for Indra.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अध॒ यदि॒मे प॑वमान॒ रोद॑सी इ॒मा च॒ विश्वा॒ भुव॑ना॒भि  ॒॑
> मज्मना ।  
> यू॒थे न नि॒ष्ठा वृ॑ष॒भो वि ति॑ष्ठसे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अध । यत् , इमे इति । पवमान ! रोदसी इति . इमा च । विश्वा । भुवना ।  
> अभि । मज्जना ।  
> यूथे । न निःस्था वृषभः । वि । तिष्ठसे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे पवमान सोम अधानंतरं यद्यथेमे रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्याविमेमानि विश्वा विश्वानि भुवना भूतजातानि च मज्मना बलेनाभि तिष्ठसि सत्वं तथा कुर्वन्
> भुवनेषु वि तिष्ठसे । तत्र दृष्टांतः यूथे न यथा कश्चिद्वृषभो गवां यूथे
> वृंदे निष्का निष्मितो वर्तते तद्वदूथस्थानीयेषु भूतजातेषु निष्मितो भवसि !

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O purifying Soma, thereafter, just as you stand over (*abhi tiṣṭhasi*) these two worlds, heaven and earth, and all these beings by your might (मज्मना (*majmanā*)), so, doing thus, you stand in different ways among the beings. The example: as a bull stands established in a herd of cows, so you are established among the classes of beings, which are like the herd.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पवमान (*pavamāna*)—O Soma
- वृषभः (*vṛṣabhaḥ*)—a powerful bull
- यूथे (*yūthe*)—in a herd of cows
- निष्ठाः न (*niṣṭhāḥ na*)—as it stands exercising mastery
- अध (*adha*)—as soon as you are born
- यत् (*yat*)—in the way that
- इमे रोदसी (*ime rodasī*)—these two, heaven and earth
- इमा च विश्वा भुवना (*imā ca viśvā bhuvanā*)—in all these worlds
- मज्मना (*majmanā*)—by their power
- अभि (*abhi*)—you make them stand pervading, in that way also
- वि तिष्ठसे (*vi tiṣṭhase*)—you stand exercising mastery

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, just as a powerful bull stands exercising mastery in a herd of cows, so, as soon as you are born, in the way you make these two, heaven and earth, and all these worlds stand pervaded by their own power, in that way too you stand exercising mastery.

### Printed p. 132

##### English translation
Since, Pavamana, you by your might are chief over these two worlds and all these living beings, as a bull is ruler in a herd of cows.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सोमः॑ पुना॒नो अ॒व्यये॒ वारे॒ शिशु॒र्न क्रीळ॒न्नव॑मानो  
> अक्षाः ।  
> स॒हस्र॑धारः श॒तवा॑ज॒ इंदुः॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सोमः । पुनानः । अव्यये । वारे । शिशुः । न । क्रीळन् । पवमानः । अक्षारिति ।  
> सहस्रधारः । शतनाजः । इंदुः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुनानः पूयमानः पवमानः पूतः सोमोऽव्ययेऽविमये वारे वाले
> पवित्रे शिशुर्न शिशुरिव क्रीळन्नितस्ततः संक्रीडमानः सन्नक्षाः । क्षरति । कीदृशः । सहस्रधारो बहुधारायुक्तः शतवाजो बहुबल इंदुर्दिप्तः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, being purified and cleansed, flows (अक्षाः (*akṣāḥ*)) through the woollen (अव्यये (*avyaye*)) filter (वारे (*vāre*)), playing here and there like a child. What is he like? Having a thousand streams, having hundredfold strength (शतवाजः (*śatavājaḥ*)), and bright (इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—the purifier
- पवमानः (*pavamānaḥ*)—one who has attained purity
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- सहस्रधारः (*sahasradhāraḥ*)—having many streams
- शतवाजः (*śatavājaḥ*)—having abundant strength
- इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*)—shining and radiant
- शिशुः (*śiśuḥ*)—a young child
- क्रीळन् न (*krīḷan na*)—as it plays
- अव्यये (*avyaye*)—of wool
- वारे (*vāre*)—through the fibres
- अक्षाः (*akṣāḥ*)—flows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma, the purifier, who has attained purity, having many streams, abundant strength and shining, flows through the fibres of wool as a young child plays.

### Printed p. 133

##### English translation
Pavamana Soma sporting like a child drops filtered through the woollen fleece, thousand-streamed, having the might of a hundred, radiant.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒ष पु॑ना॒नो मधु॑मा ऋ॒तावेंद्रा॒येंदुः॑ पवते स्वा॒दुरू॒र्मिः ।  
> वा॒ज॒सनि॑र्वरिवो॒विद्व॑यो॒धाः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषः । पुनानः । मधुमान् । ऋतवा । इंद्राय । इंदु: पवते : स्वादुः  
> ऊर्मिः ।  
> वाजसनिः वरिवः वित् । वयःधाः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुनानः पूयमानो मधुमान् माधुर्योपेत ऋतवर्तवान् यज्ञवानिंदुः क्षरणशीलः स्वादुः स्वादुकर ऊर्मिरेष रसधारासंघ इंद्रायेंद्रार्थं
> पवते । क्षरति । कीदृशः । वाजसनिरन्नस्य दाता वरिवोविद्वनस्य लंभको वयोधा
> आयुषो दाता ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, being purified, is sweet, possessed of ṛta (the sacrifice), flowing and delicious; this wave, a stream of juice, flows for Indra's sake. He is described as the giver of food (वाजसनिः (*vājasaniḥ*)), the obtainer of wealth (वरिवोवित् (*varivovit*)) and the giver of life (वयोधाः (*vayodhāḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—the purifier
- मधुमान् (*madhumān*)—endowed with sweetness
- ऋतवा (*ṛtavā*)—possessing the sacrifice
- इंदुः (*iṃduḥ*)—of flowing nature
- स्वादुः (*svāduḥ*)—delicious
- ऊर्मिः (*ūrmiḥ*)—possessing waves
- एषः (*eṣaḥ*)—this Soma
- वाजसनिः (*vājasaniḥ*)—the giver of food
- वरिवोवित् (*varivovit*)—the giver of wealth
- वयोधाः (*vayodhāḥ*)—the giver of life
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—for Indra's drinking
- पवते (*pavate*)—flows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Soma, who is the purifier, sweet, possessed of the sacrifice, flowing, delicious and wave-bearing, flows, as giver of food, wealth and life, for Indra to drink.

### Printed p. 134

##### English translation
This Soma juice undergoing purification, sweet-flavoured, holy, dropping, flows for Indra a sweet stream, bestowing food, dispensing wealth and life.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स प॑वस्व॒ सह॑मानः पृत॒न्यून्तेध॒न्रक्षां॒स्यप॑ दु॒र्गहा॑णि ।  
> स्वा॒यु॒धः सा॑स॒हवान्त्सो॑म॒ शत्रु॑न् ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । पवस्व । सहमानः । पृतन्यून् । सेधन् । रक्षांसि । अप । दुःगहानि ।  
> सुआयुधः । ससहवान् । सोम । शत्रून् ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम स तादृशस्त्वं पवस्व । किं कुर्वन् । पृतन्यून् ॥ कव्यध्वरपृतनस्यर्चि
> लोप इति क्यचि परतोऽकारलोपः ॥ संग्रामकामाञ्त्यून् सहमानोऽभिभवन् तथा दुर्गहाणि कैश्चितपि दुर्गमाणि रक्षांस्यप सेधन्नपगमयन् हिंसन् ।
> किंच स्वायुधः शोभनायधः सञ्त्यून्सासह्वानभिभवन्नभितपन् पवस्व ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Soma, such as you are, flow." What doing? Overcoming (सहमानः (*sahamānaḥ*)) the पृतन्यून् (*pṛtanyūn*), those desiring battle (the elision of the a before kyac is by the rule he cites), and driving away (अप सेधन् (*apa sedhan*)) the demons hard for anyone to approach. Moreover, well-armed, mastering the enemies, flow, afflicting them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सोम (*soma*)—O Soma
- सः (*saḥ*)—you, the renowned
- पृतन्यून् (*pṛtanyūn*)—the enemies who desire battle
- सहमानः (*sahamānaḥ*)—defeating
- दुर्गहाणि (*durgahāṇi*)—unassailable
- रक्षांसि (*rakṣāṃsi*)—the demons
- अप सेधन् (*apa sedhan*)—destroying
- स्वायुधः (*svāyudhaḥ*)—having excellent weapons
- शत्रून् (*śatrūn*)—all enemies
- सासहवान् (*sāsahavān*)—wholly defeating
- पवस्व (*pavasva*)—flow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Soma, renowned as you are, flow, defeating the enemies who desire battle, destroying the unassailable demons, well-armed, and wholly defeating all enemies.

##### English translation
Flow, Soma, overcoming (our) enemies, driving away the Rakshasas who are difficult to approach, well-armed, triumphing over foes.

### Printed p. 135
## Sūkta 111 (Maṇḍala 9)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अया रुचेति तृचमष्टमं सूक्तं परुच्छेपपुत्रस्यानानताख्यस्यार्षमत्यष्टिच्छंदस्कं । पवमानसोमदेवताकं । तथा चानुक्रम्यते । अया रुचा तृचमनानतः पारुच्छेपिरात्यष्टमिति ॥ गतो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The eighth sūkta, अया रुचा (*ayā rucā*), a tṛca, has as its seer Anānata, son of Parucchepa; its deity is Pavamāna Soma; its metre is Atyaṣṭi. The Anukramaṇī says: अया रुचा तृचमनानतः पारुच्छेपिरात्यष्टमिति (*ayā rucā tṛcamanānataḥ pārucchepirātyaṣṭamiti*). The application (viniyoga) has been stated before.

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning अया रुचा (*ayā rucā*) is the eighth sūkta in the seventh anuvāka; it has three ṛks. Its seer is Anānata, son of the seer Parucchepa; the deity is Pavamāna Soma; the metre is Atyaṣṭi. In the Anukramaṇī it is said: अया रुचा तृचमनानतः पारुच्छेपिरात्यष्टं (*ayā rucā tṛcamanānataḥ pārucchepirātyaṣṭaṃ*). The application of the sūkta has been stated earlier.

Sūkta 111. Maṇḍala 9, Anuvāka 7; Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 5, Varga 24. Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 3. Seer: Anānata Pārucchepi. Deity: Pavamāna Soma. Metre: Atyaṣṭi.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒या रु॒चा हरि॑णा पुना॒नो विश्वा॒ द्वेषां॑सि तरति स्व॒युग्व॑भिः॒ सूरो॒ न स्व॒युग्व॑भिः ।  
> धारा॑ सु॒तस्य॑ रोचते पुना॒नो अ॑रु॒षो हरिः॑ ।  
> विश्वा॒ यद्रू॒पा प॑रि॒यात्यृक्व॑भिः स॒प्तास्ये॑भि॒र्ऋक्व॑भिः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अया । रुचा । हरिण्या । पुनानः । विश्वा । द्वेषांसि । तरति । स्वयुग्वभिः । सूरः ।  
> न । स्वयुग्वभिः ।  
> धारा । सुतस्य । रोचते । पुनानः . अरुषः । हरिः ।  
> विश्वा । यत् । रूपा । परियाति । यक्वभिः । सप्तआस्येभिः । यक्वभिः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 136

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुनानः पूयमानः सोमो हरिण्या हरितवर्णयायानया रुचा रोचमानया धारया विश्वा सर्वाणि द्वेषांसि द्वेष्टृणि रक्षांसि तरति । विनाशयति । तत्र
> दृष्टांतः । सूरो न यथा सूर्यः स्वयुग्वभिः स्वयं युक्तै रश्मिभिस्तमांसि
> हिनस्ति तद्वत् । स्वयुग्वभिरिति द्विरुक्तिरादरार्था । यद्वा । धारया युक्तः सोमः
> स्वीय्यर्युक्तैस्तेजोभी रक्षांसि तरति । तस्य सुतस्याभिसुतस्य सोमस्य धारा
> रोचते । दीप्यते । पुनानः पूयमानो हरिर्हरितवर्णः सोमोऽरुष आरोचमानो भवति । यद्यः सोमः सप्तास्येभीरसहरणशीलास्यॆर्ऋक्वभिः स्तुतिमद्भिर्युक्वभिस्तेजोभिर्विश्वा विश्वानि व्याप्तानि रूपा रूपाणि नक्षत्राणि परियाति गच्छति
> व्याप्नोति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Soma, being purified, with this shining green-tinted stream overcomes (destroys) all hateful demons. The example: as the sun destroys darkness with its self-yoked rays, so he does; the repetition स्वयुग्वभिः (*svayugvabhiḥ*) is for emphasis. Or else: Soma, joined with the stream, overcomes the demons with his own yoked energies. The stream of the pressed Soma shines; being purified, the green Soma becomes radiant. Or: that Soma, with the seven-mouthed, juice-bearing, praise-accompanied ऋक्वभिः (*ṛkvabhiḥ*) (energies), goes about and pervades all forms, the constellations.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सूरः (*sūraḥ*)—the sun
- स्वयुग्वभिः न (*svayugvabhiḥ na*)—as he destroys darkness with rays born of himself
- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—Soma, who is being purified
- हरिण्या (*hariṇyā*)—green-hued
- अया रुचा (*ayā rucā*)—with this radiance
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all
- द्वेषांसि (*dveṣāṃsi*)—enemies
- स्वयुग्वभिः (*svayugvabhiḥ*)—with his own energies
- तरति (*tarati*)—destroys
- सुतस्य (*sutasya*)—of the pressed Soma
- धारा (*dhārā*)—the stream
- रोचते (*rocate*)—shines
- यत् (*yat*)—which Soma
- सप्तास्येभिः (*saptāsyebhiḥ*)—with seven (juice-bearing) mouths
- ऋक्वभिः (*ṛkvabhiḥ*)—accompanied by praises
- ऋक्वभिः (*ṛkvabhiḥ*)—with energies
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all-pervading
- रूपा (*rūpā*)—forms (constellations etc.)
- परियाति (*pariyāti*)—possesses
- पुनानः (*punānaḥ*)—being purified
- हरिः (*hariḥ*)—green-hued
- अरुषः (*aruṣaḥ*)—is radiant (golden)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As the sun destroys darkness with rays born of himself, so Soma, being purified, destroys all enemies with this green-hued radiance. The stream of the pressed Soma juice shines. That Soma which possesses, with its juice-bearing, seven-mouthed energies accompanied by praises, the all-pervading forms such as the constellations, being purified and green-hued, is radiant.

##### English translation
With this bright green-tinted stream Soma being filtered overcomes all enemies with the (juices) yoked to it, as the sun (overcomes the darkness) with the (rays) yoked to it ; the stream of the effused (Soma) shines, the filtered green-tinted (Soma is) resplendent, which pervades all constellations, with the Rikvans, with the seven-mouthed Rikvans.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं त्यत्प॑णी॒नां वि॑दो॒ वस्तु॒ सं मा॒तृभि॑र्मर्जयसि॒ स्व आ  
> दम॑ ऋ॒तस्य॑ धी॒तिभि॒र्दमॆ  ॑
> प॒रा॒वतो॒ न साम॒ तद्यत्रा॒ रणं॑ति धी॒तयः॑ ।  
> त्रि॒धातु॑भि॒ररु॑षीभि॒र्वयो॑ दधॆ:॒ रोच॑मानो॒ वयो॑ दधे ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं : त्यत् । पणीनां । विदः । वसु । सं । मातृभिः । मर्जयसि । स्वे । आ । दमे ।  
> ऋतस्य । धीतिभिः । दमे ।  
> परावतः । न । साम । तत् । यत्र । रणंति । धीतयः ।  
> त्रिधातुभिः । अरुषीभिः । वयः । दधे । रोचमानः । वयः । दधे ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सोम त्वं तृप्तिणीनां वसु पणिभिरपहृतं गवात्मकं धन, विदः । अलभथाः । आ अपि चर्तस्य यज्ञस्य धीतिभिर्धात्रीभिर्मातृभिर्वसतीवरीभिः स्व आत्मीये
> दमे गृहभूते दमे यज्ञे सं मर्जयसि । सम्यक् शुद्धो भवसि । परावतो नॆ
> दूरस्थाद्देशाद्यथा साम सामध्वनिः श्रूयते तथा तव तत्सामध्वनिः सर्वॆः श्रूयते । असौ सोमाभिषवाभिप्रायेणोक्तः । यत्र यस्मिञ्चद्भीतयः कर्मणो
> धर्तारो यजमाना रणंति रमंते । रोचमानः सोमस्त्रिधातुभिस्तर्याणां
> लोकानां धारयित्रिभिर्मातृभिर्व सतीवरीभिररुषीभिरारोचमानाभिर्दिप्तिर्वयोS
> न्नं दधे । स्तोतृभ्यः प्रयच्छति । पुनर्वयो दध इत्यादरार्थ० ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Soma, you found the wealth of the Paṇis, the wealth of cattle carried off by them. And you are well purified in your own house, the sacrifice, by the mothers, the supporting waters (वसतीवरीभिः (*vasatīvarībhiḥ*)) of the sacrifice. As the sound of a sāman is heard from a distant place, so that sound of yours is heard by all; this is said with reference to the pressing of Soma, in which the supporters of the rite, the sacrificers, rejoice. Shining Soma, with the radiant waters that sustain the three worlds, bestows food (वयः (*vayaḥ*)) on the praisers; the repetition वयो दधे (*vayo dadhe*) is for emphasis. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you (O Soma)
- तृत् (*tṛt*)—renowned **NOTE**
- पणीनां (*paṇīnāṃ*)—of the Paṇis (carried off by them)
- वसु (*vasu*)—wealth consisting of cattle
- विदः (*vidaḥ*)—you obtain
- आ (*ā*)—and
